    42→
    43→<objective>
    44→Wire BYO pinning config into SDK client lifecycle and add source unpin to migration worker
    45→
    46→Purpose: Two critical gaps prevent BYO-IPFS from working end-to-end: (1) the SDK client is created at login without pinningConfig, so pinWithMode() always uses cipherbox-only mode regardless of saved BYO settings, and (2) the TEE migration worker copies CIDs but never unpins from the source provider, leaving orphaned pins.
    47→
    48→Output: SDK client initialized with BYO config at login, duplicate uploads respect pinning mode, migration cleans up source pins after verified transfer.

   240→  <action>
   241→    **Problem:** The migration worker in `migrateBatch()` copies CIDs from source to destination and verifies CID integrity (line 184), but after verification succeeds it only pushes to `succeeded[]` without unpinning from the source. This leaves orphaned pins on the source provider.
   242→
   243→    **Fix -- Add unpinFromProvider function and call after CID verification:**
   244→
   245→    1. Add a new `async function unpinFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<void>` after the existing `pinToProvider` function:
   246→
   247→    ```typescript
   248→    async function unpinFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<void> {
   249→      const token = authTokenString(config);
   250→
   251→      if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   252→        const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   253→        if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   254→        const response = await fetch(
   255→          `${config.endpoint}/api/v0/pin/rm?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   256→          {
   257→            method: 'POST',
   258→            headers,
   259→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   260→          }
   261→        );
   262→        if (!response.ok) {
   263→          throw new Error(`Unpin from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   264→        }
   265→        return;
   266→      }
   267→
   268→      if (config.protocol === 'psa') {
   269→        // PSA requires finding the requestid first, then deleting
   270→        const listResponse = await fetch(
   271→          `${config.endpoint}/pins?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}&status=pinned,pinning,queued`,
   272→          {
   273→            method: 'GET',
   274→            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   275→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   276→          }
   277→        );
   278→        if (!listResponse.ok) return; // Best-effort: if list fails, skip unpin
   279→        const listResult = (await listResponse.json()) as { results: Array<{ requestid: string }> };
   280→        for (const pin of listResult.results) {
   281→          await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins/${encodeURIComponent(pin.requestid)}`, {
   282→            method: 'DELETE',
   283→            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   284→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   285→          });
   286→        }
   287→        return;
   288→      }
   289→
   290→      // 'cipherbox' protocol: unpin via CipherBox API -- not handled in TEE worker.
   291→      // CipherBox unpins are handled by the API-side MigrationProcessor.
   292→    }
   293→    ```
   294→
   295→    2. In the `migrateBatch()` function, after `succeeded.push(cid)` (line 188), add a best-effort source unpin:
   296→
   297→    ```typescript
   298→    succeeded.push(cid);
   299→
   300→    // Best-effort source unpin after verified transfer
   301→    // Failure here is non-fatal -- the CID is already on the destination
   302→    try {
   303→      if (sourceConfig.protocol !== 'cipherbox') {
   304→        await unpinFromProvider(cid, sourceConfig);
   305→      }
   306→    } catch {
   307→      // Source unpin failure is non-fatal; CID is safely on destination
   308→    }
   309→    ```
   310→
   311→    The `sourceConfig.protocol !== 'cipherbox'` guard ensures we don't try to unpin from CipherBox via the TEE worker -- CipherBox unpins are handled separately by the API-side MigrationProcessor which has direct access to the IPFS provider.
   312→
   313→    3. Do NOT unpin CIDs from the `failed[]` array -- those were not successfully transferred and must remain on the source.
   314→

    46→<objective>
    47→Add TEE-routed connection test endpoint to avoid browser CORS and credential exposure issues
    48→
    49→Purpose: The current connection test runs in the browser, which means: (a) CORS blocks many SaaS providers that don't set Access-Control-Allow-Origin, (b) auth tokens are used directly in browser fetch requests. By routing through the TEE, credentials are ECIES-encrypted before leaving the browser, decrypted only in the TEE enclave, and the TEE probes the endpoint server-side where CORS doesn't apply.
    50→
    51→Output: POST /tee/connection-test API endpoint, TEE worker /connection-test route, updated ConnectionTest component using server-side testing.
    52→</objective>

   157→       - Get TEE keypair for the epoch: `const keypair = await getKeypair(body.epoch)`
   158→       - Decrypt: `const configBytes = decrypt(keypair.privateKey, Buffer.from(body.encryptedConfig, 'hex'))`
   159→       - Zero TEE private key: `keypair.privateKey.fill(0)`
   160→       - Parse config: `const { endpoint, authToken } = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(configBytes))`

   234→    @Post('connection-test')

   236→    @ApiOperation({

   136→    **1. Update types.ts to add 'pinata' protocol:**
   137→

   139→
   140→    Also update `ConnectionTestResult.protocol` from `'kubo' | 'psa'` to `'kubo' | 'psa' | 'pinata'`.
   141→
   142→    **2. Create `packages/sdk-core/src/pinning/pinata-provider.ts`:**
   143→
   144→    Pinata API reference (v3):
   145→    - **Upload file:** `POST https://uploads.pinata.cloud/v3/files` with multipart form data. Header: `Authorization: Bearer <JWT>`. Response: `{ data: { id: string, cid: string, size: number } }`
   146→    - **Pin by CID (existing content):** `POST https://api.pinata.cloud/pinning/pinByHash` with `{ hashToPin: cid }`. Header: `Authorization: Bearer <JWT>`. Response: `{ id: string, ipfsHash: string, status: string }`
   147→    - **List files:** `GET https://api.pinata.cloud/v3/files?cid=<cid>`. Response: `{ data: { files: [{ id, cid, size }] } }`
   148→    - **Unpin file:** `DELETE https://api.pinata.cloud/v3/files/<id>`. Response: 200 OK
   149→    - **Get file:** `GET https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>` -- Pinata provides a dedicated gateway per account
   150→
   151→    Note: Pinata uses TWO base URLs:
   152→    - `https://uploads.pinata.cloud` for file uploads
   153→    - `https://api.pinata.cloud` for management operations (list, delete, pinByHash)
   154→
   155→    The `endpoint` config should be `https://api.pinata.cloud` (the management endpoint). Uploads always go to the fixed `https://uploads.pinata.cloud`.
   156→
   157→    Implementation:
   158→
   159→    ```typescript
   160→    import type { PinningProvider, PinResult, PinStatus } from './types';
   161→
   162→    const UPLOAD_URL = 'https://uploads.pinata.cloud';
   163→    const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000;
   164→
   165→    export class PinataProvider implements PinningProvider {
   166→      private readonly endpoint: string; // https://api.pinata.cloud
   167→      private readonly authToken: string; // Pinata JWT
   168→      private readonly gatewayUrl?: string; // Optional dedicated gateway
   169→
   170→      constructor(endpoint: string, authToken: string, gatewayUrl?: string) {
   171→        this.endpoint = endpoint.replace(/\/+$/, '');
   172→        this.authToken = authToken;
   173→        this.gatewayUrl = gatewayUrl?.replace(/\/+$/, '');
   174→      }
   175→
   176→      async pin(data: Uint8Array, name?: string): Promise<PinResult> {
   177→        const blob = new Blob([data]);
   178→        const formData = new FormData();
   179→        formData.append('file', blob, name ?? `cipherbox-${Date.now()}`);
   180→
   181→        const response = await fetch(`${UPLOAD_URL}/v3/files`, {
   182→          method: 'POST',
   183→          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}` },
   184→          body: formData,
   185→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   186→        });
   187→
   188→        if (!response.ok) {
   189→          const errorText = await response.text();
   190→          throw new Error(`Pinata upload failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
   191→        }
   192→
   193→        const result = (await response.json()) as {
   194→          data: { id: string; cid: string; size: number };
   195→        };
   196→        return { cid: result.data.cid, size: result.data.size };
   197→      }
   198→
   199→      async pinByCid(cid: string): Promise<{ cid: string; status: string }> {
   200→        const response = await fetch(`${this.endpoint}/pinning/pinByHash`, {
   201→          method: 'POST',
   202→          headers: {
   203→            Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}`,
   204→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   205→          },
   206→          body: JSON.stringify({ hashToPin: cid }),
   207→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   208→        });
   209→
   210→        if (!response.ok) {
   211→          const errorText = await response.text();
   212→          throw new Error(`Pinata pinByHash failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
   213→        }
   214→
   215→        const result = (await response.json()) as { ipfsHash: string; status: string };
   216→        return { cid: result.ipfsHash, status: result.status };
   217→      }
   218→
   219→      async unpin(cid: string): Promise<void> {
   220→        // Find the file ID by CID first
   221→        const listResponse = await fetch(
   222→          `${this.endpoint}/v3/files?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   223→          {
   224→            method: 'GET',
   225→            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}` },
   226→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   227→          }
   228→        );
   229→
   230→        if (!listResponse.ok) {
   231→          throw new Error(`Pinata list failed: ${listResponse.status}`);
   232→        }
   233→
   234→        const listResult = (await listResponse.json()) as {
   235→          data: { files: Array<{ id: string }> };
   236→        };
   237→
   238→        for (const file of listResult.data.files) {
   239→          const deleteResponse = await fetch(`${this.endpoint}/v3/files/${file.id}`, {
   240→            method: 'DELETE',
   241→            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}` },
   242→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   243→          });
   244→          if (!deleteResponse.ok) {
   245→            throw new Error(`Pinata delete failed: ${deleteResponse.status}`);
   246→          }
   247→        }
   248→      }
   249→
   250→      async status(cid: string): Promise<PinStatus> {
   251→        const response = await fetch(
   252→          `${this.endpoint}/v3/files?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   253→          {
   254→            method: 'GET',
   255→            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.authToken}` },
   256→            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   257→          }
   258→        );
   259→
   260→        if (!response.ok) return { cid, status: 'failed' };
   261→
   262→        const result = (await response.json()) as {
   263→          data: { files: Array<{ id: string; cid: string }> };
   264→        };
   265→
   266→        return {
   267→          cid,
   268→          status: result.data.files.length > 0 ? 'pinned' : 'failed',
   269→        };
   270→      }
   271→
   272→      async get(cid: string): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   273→        const gateway = this.gatewayUrl ?? 'https://gateway.pinata.cloud';
   274→        const response = await fetch(`${gateway}/ipfs/${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   275→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS),
   276→        });
   277→
   278→        if (!response.ok) {
   279→          throw new Error(`Pinata gateway fetch failed: ${response.status}`);
   280→        }
   281→
   282→        return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   283→      }
   284→
   285→      // Expose auth token format for connection test detection
   286→      static readonly AUTH_HEADER_PREFIX = 'Bearer';
   287→    }
   288→    ```

   325→    Add a `probePinata` function after the existing `probePsa` function:
   326→
   327→    ```typescript
   328→    /** Probe Pinata API via GET /data/testAuthentication */
   329→    async function probePinata(
   330→      endpoint: string,
   331→      authToken?: string
   332→    ): Promise<ConnectionTestResult | null> {
   333→      // Pinata detection: try the test authentication endpoint
   334→      const url = `${endpoint}/data/testAuthentication`;
   335→      const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   336→      if (authToken) {
   337→        headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${authToken}`;
   338→      }
   339→
   340→      const start = performance.now();
   341→
   342→      try {
   343→        const response = await fetch(url, {
   344→          method: 'GET',
   345→          headers,
   346→          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   347→        });
   348→
   349→        const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   350→
   351→        if (response.ok) {
   352→          return {
   353→            success: true,
   354→            protocol: 'pinata',
   355→            version: 'Pinata API v3',
   356→            latencyMs,
   357→          };
   358→        }
   359→
   360→        if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
   361→          return {
   362→            success: false,
   363→            protocol: 'pinata',
   364→            latencyMs,
   365→            error: 'authentication failed. check your pinata jwt token.',
   366→          };
   367→        }
   368→
   369→        return null;
   370→      } catch (err) {
   371→        const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   372→
   373→        if (isCorsError(err)) {
   374→          // Pinata API should support CORS, but if not:
   375→          return {
   376→            success: false,
   377→            latencyMs,
   378→            corsError: true,
   379→            corsInstructions: 'pinata api should support browser requests. check if your jwt token has the correct permissions.',
   380→            error: 'CORS error when connecting to pinata.',
   381→          };
   382→        }
   383→
   384→        return null;
   385→      }
   386→    }
   387→    ```

## Wave 1

**21-08: Wire BYO pinning config into SDK client lifecycle**
SDK client is created at login without pinningConfig, so dual-pin/external modes never activate. Fix: load BYO config from encrypted IPNS entry at login, add runtime reconfiguration, add source unpin to migration worker.

**21-09: TEE-routed connection test endpoint**
Current connection test runs in browser, hitting CORS blocks on SaaS providers. Fix: route through TEE — credentials ECIES-encrypted, decrypted in-enclave, server-side probe eliminates CORS.

**21-10: PinataProvider for Pinata native API**
Pinata deprecated their PSA endpoint. Need dedicated provider using Pinata's v3 API for upload, pinByHash for CID-based pinning, and auto-detection in connection test.

Spawning 3 agents in parallel...

> AGENT

Here's the setup:

1. **Create a Pinata account** at https://www.pinata.cloud/ (free tier works)

2. **Create an API key:**
   - Go to **API Keys** in the Pinata dashboard
   - Click **New Key**
   - Give it **Admin** permissions (or at minimum: pinning, file uploads, file listing)
   - Copy the **JWT** token (the long one, not the API key/secret pair)

3. **Set the env vars in this terminal** — run this with your JWT filled in:
   ```bash
   ! export BYO_IPFS_ENDPOINT=https://api.pinata.cloud
   ! export BYO_IPFS_AUTH_TOKEN=<paste-your-pinata-jwt-here>
   ! export BYO_IPFS_PROTOCOL=pinata
   ! export BYO_IPFS_PROVIDER_NAME=Pinata
   ```
   (Use the `!` prefix so they land in this session)

4. **Verify it works:**
   ```bash
   ! curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $BYO_IPFS_AUTH_TOKEN" https://api.pinata.cloud/data/testAuthentication
   ```
   Should return `{"message":"Congratulations! You are communicating with the Pinata API!"}` or similar.

Once verified, say "provider ready" and I'll spawn the benchmarking agent.

- BYO adds +47% median latency vs local Kubo but improves tail latency (p99 -13.5%)

    90→## Context
    91→
    92→**Current State (v1.0 shipped 2026-03-05):**
    93→
    94→- 423,869 lines of TypeScript + Rust across 698 source files
    95→- NestJS API, React 18 web app, Tauri desktop (macOS/Windows/Linux)
    96→- 155 plans executed across 35 phase directories (M1 + M2)
    97→- Staging deployed at api-staging.cipherbox.cc / app-staging.cipherbox.cc
    98→- 8 Playwright E2E test suites + 4 desktop E2E script pairs
    99→
   100→**Technical Environment:**
   101→
   102→- IPFS via Kubo for file storage and pinning
   103→- IPNS for mutable metadata pointers (per-folder + per-file)
   104→- Web3Auth Core Kit MPC for deterministic ECDSA key derivation
   105→- Phala Cloud for TEE auto-republishing (3-hour interval)
   106→
   107→**Key Architecture (evolved through M1 + M2):**
   108→
   109→- Client-side encryption only — server is zero-knowledge relay
   110→- Per-folder + per-file IPNS keypairs (HKDF-derived)
   111→- ECIES key re-wrapping for zero-knowledge sharing
   112→- Optimistic concurrency on IPNS publishes (sequence number checks)
   113→- Deterministic vault IPNS derivation (self-sovereign recovery)
   114→- FUSE-T SMB backend on macOS (NFS had kernel bugs)
   115→
   116→## Constraints
   117→
   118→- **File size**: 100 MB max — browser memory limits
   119→- **Storage quota**: 500 MiB free tier — IPFS storage management
   120→- **Files per folder**: 1,000 max — UI performance
   121→- **Folder depth**: 20 levels max — traversal performance
   122→- **Sync latency**: ~30 seconds — IPNS polling interval
   123→- **Tech stack**: NestJS backend, React 18 frontend, Tauri desktop — per specifications
   124→- **Auth provider**: Web3Auth Core Kit MPC — deterministic key derivation requirement
   125→- **IPFS provider**: Kubo (self-hosted)
   126→
   127→## Key Decisions
   128→
   129→| Decision                                    | Rationale                                                  | Outcome |
   130→| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------- |
   131→| Full-stack vertical build order             | Test features end-to-end as they're built                  | Good    |
   132→| Web + cross-platform desktop for v1.0       | Complete user experience across all platforms              | Good    |
   133→| TEE republishing required for v1.0          | Zero-downtime vault access guarantee                       | Good    |
   134→| Core Kit MPC replaces PnP Modal             | Self-hosted identity provider, MFA foundation              | Good    |
   135→| Per-file IPNS metadata split                | Decouple content updates from folder publishes             | Good    |
   136→| AES-256-CTR for streaming media             | Byte-range decryption enables in-browser playback          | Good    |
   137→| Optimistic concurrency via sequence numbers | Lightweight conflict detection without distributed locks   | Good    |
   138→| FUSE-T SMB backend (not NFS)                | macOS NFS kernel bug blocked WRITE RPCs for new files      | Good    |
   139→| Encrypted recycle bin on IPFS               | Client-side retention enforcement, CID unpinning on delete | Good    |
   140→| SIWE wallet login with hashed identifiers   | Privacy-preserving auth, unified identity across methods   | Good    |
   141→| Decimal phase numbering for insertions      | Clear insertion semantics without renumbering              | Good    |
   142→

     1→/**
     2→ * @cipherbox/core - Vault Types
     3→ *
     4→ * Type definitions for vault initialization and encrypted key storage.
     5→ */
     6→
     7→import type { Ed25519Keypair } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     8→
     9→/**
    10→ * Result of vault initialization (plaintext, in-memory only).
    11→ *
    12→ * These keys are NEVER persisted to storage - they exist only in memory.
    13→ * When the user logs out or refreshes, keys are re-derived from Web3Auth
    14→ * or wallet signature.
    15→ */
    16→export type VaultInit = {
    17→  /** 32-byte AES key for root folder encryption */
    18→  rootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
    19→  /** Ed25519 keypair for signing root IPNS records */
    20→  rootIpnsKeypair: Ed25519Keypair;
    21→};
    22→
    23→/**
    24→ * Vault keys encrypted for server storage (zero-knowledge).
    25→ *
    26→ * The server stores these encrypted blobs without any knowledge of
    27→ * the plaintext keys. Only the user's ECIES private key can decrypt them.
    28→ *
    29→ * The IPNS public key is NOT stored -- it is derived from the IPNS private
    30→ * key after decryption (deterministic Ed25519 derivation).
    31→ */
    32→export type EncryptedVaultKeys = {
    33→  /** Root folder key ECIES-wrapped with user's publicKey */
    34→  encryptedRootFolderKey: Uint8Array;
    35→  /** IPNS private key ECIES-wrapped with user's publicKey */
    36→  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    37→};
    38→
    39→/**
    40→ * Vault key blob v2 is now a simple binary envelope: 0x02 | uint16_BE(key_len) | encrypted_key
    41→ *
    42→ * deserializeVaultBlobV2 returns the ECIES-encrypted rootFolderKey directly as Uint8Array.
    43→ * No separate type needed.
    44→ */
    45→
    46→/**
    47→ * BYO-IPFS configuration stored in vault metadata on IPFS.
    48→ * Encrypted with user's key, decrypted client-side only.
    49→ * Server never sees this data (zero-knowledge preserved).
    50→ *
    51→ * Default when absent: { pinningMode: 'cipherbox', externalProvider: null }
    52→ */
    53→export type ByoIpfsConfig = {
    54→  /** User-selected pinning mode */
    55→  pinningMode: 'cipherbox' | 'external' | 'dual';
    56→  /** External provider config (null when mode is 'cipherbox') */
    57→  externalProvider: {
    58→    endpoint: string;
    59→    authToken: string;
    60→    protocol: 'psa' | 'kubo';
    61→    providerName?: string;
    62→  } | null;
    63→};

     1→import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { useAuthStore } from '../../stores/auth.store';
     3→import { useQuotaStore } from '../../stores/quota.store';
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Format bytes to human-readable string.
     7→ */
     8→function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
     9→  if (bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
    10→
    11→  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
    12→  const k = 1024;
    13→  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    14→  const value = bytes / Math.pow(k, i);
    15→
    16→  // Use 1 decimal place for larger units
    17→  return `${value.toFixed(i > 1 ? 1 : 0)} ${units[i]}`;
    18→}
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Storage quota indicator component.
    22→ * Shows storage usage with a progress bar and text.
    23→ * Fetches current quota from the backend on mount.
    24→ * Displays advisory badge for BYO users.
    25→ */
    26→export function StorageQuota() {
    27→  const { usedBytes, limitBytes, advisory, fetchQuota } = useQuotaStore();
    28→  const isAuthenticated = useAuthStore((s) => s.isAuthenticated);
    29→
    30→  // Fetch quota from backend once authenticated so the display reflects
    31→  // actual usage (e.g. files uploaded in previous sessions).
    32→  // Guarded by isAuthenticated to avoid firing before session restoration
    33→  // completes — an unauthenticated request would race with the refresh
    34→  // token flow and could trigger an accidental logout.
    35→  useEffect(() => {
    36→    if (isAuthenticated) {
    37→      fetchQuota();
    38→    }
    39→  }, [fetchQuota, isAuthenticated]);
    40→
    41→  // Calculate percentage (avoid division by zero)
    42→  const percentage = limitBytes > 0 ? (usedBytes / limitBytes) * 100 : 0;
    43→
    44→  return (
    45→    <div className="storage-quota" data-testid="storage-quota">
    46→      <div className="storage-quota-bar">
    47→        <div className="storage-quota-fill" style={{ width: `${Math.min(percentage, 100)}%` }} />
    48→      </div>
    49→      <div className="storage-quota-info">
    50→        <span className="storage-quota-text">
    51→          {formatBytes(usedBytes)} / {formatBytes(limitBytes)}
    52→        </span>
    53→        {advisory && <span className="storage-quota-advisory-badge">ADVISORY</span>}
    54→      </div>
    55→      {advisory && (
    56→        <p className="storage-quota-advisory-hint">
    57→          {'storage is managed by your node. this total is approximate.'}
    58→        </p>
    59→      )}
    60→    </div>
    61→  );
    62→}
    63→

     1→/**
     2→ * Format utilities for file browser display.
     3→ */
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Format bytes into human-readable string.
     7→ *
     8→ * @param bytes - Number of bytes
     9→ * @returns Formatted string like "1.2 MB", "456 KB", etc.
    10→ *
    11→ * @example
    12→ * formatBytes(1024) // "1 KB"
    13→ * formatBytes(1536) // "1.5 KB"
    14→ * formatBytes(1048576) // "1 MB"
    15→ */
    16→export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
    17→  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
    18→
    19→  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
    20→  const k = 1024;
    21→  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    22→
    23→  // Use at most 1 decimal place, remove trailing zeros
    24→  const value = bytes / Math.pow(k, i);
    25→  const formatted = value % 1 === 0 ? value.toString() : value.toFixed(1);
    26→
    27→  return `${formatted} ${units[i]}`;
    28→}
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Format timestamp into locale-aware date string.
    32→ *
    33→ * @param timestamp - Unix timestamp in milliseconds
    34→ * @returns Formatted date string
    35→ *
    36→ * @example
    37→ * formatDate(1705852800000) // "Jan 21, 2024" (varies by locale)
    38→ */
    39→export function formatDate(timestamp: number): string {
    40→  const date = new Date(timestamp);
    41→
    42→  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    43→    year: 'numeric',
    44→    month: 'short',
    45→    day: 'numeric',
    46→  }).format(date);
    47→}
    48→
    49→/**
    50→ * Format a Unix ms timestamp as a relative time string.
    51→ *
    52→ * Returns "just now", "Xm ago", "Xh ago", "Xd ago" for recent timestamps.
    53→ * Falls back to a locale-formatted date for timestamps older than 7 days.
    54→ */
    55→export function formatRelativeTime(timestampMs: number): string {
    56→  const diff = Date.now() - timestampMs;
    57→
    58→  if (diff < 0) {
    59→    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    60→      month: 'short',
    61→      day: 'numeric',
    62→      year: 'numeric',
    63→    }).format(new Date(timestampMs));
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  if (diff < 60_000) return 'just now';
    67→  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`;
    68→  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
    69→  if (diff < 604_800_000) {
    70→    const days = Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000);
    71→    return days === 1 ? '1 day ago' : `${days} days ago`;
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    75→    month: 'short',
    76→    day: 'numeric',
    77→    year: 'numeric',
    78→  }).format(new Date(timestampMs));
    79→}
    80→
    81→/**
    82→ * Get a terminal-style type indicator based on item type and optional MIME type.
    83→ */
    84→export function getItemIcon(itemType: 'file' | 'folder', mimeType?: string): string {
    85→  if (itemType === 'folder') return '[DIR]';
    86→  if (!mimeType) return '[FILE]';
    87→  if (mimeType.startsWith('image/')) return '[IMG]';
    88→  if (mimeType.startsWith('video/')) return '[VID]';
    89→  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/')) return '[AUD]';
    90→  if (mimeType.startsWith('text/')) return '[TXT]';
    91→  if (mimeType === 'application/pdf') return '[PDF]';
    92→  return '[FILE]';
    93→}
    94→

     1→/**
     2→ * SSRF Protection Utilities
     3→ *
     4→ * Validates user-provided endpoint URLs to prevent SSRF attacks.
     5→ * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.
     6→ *
     7→ * Extracted from migration-worker.ts for reuse across TEE routes.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import { lookup } from 'node:dns/promises';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Validate endpoint URL to prevent SSRF attacks.
    14→ * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.
    15→ */
    16→export function validateEndpointUrl(endpoint: string): void {
    17→  const url = new URL(endpoint);
    18→
    19→  // Skip SSRF validation in development/simulator mode
    20→  if (process.env.TEE_MODE === 'simulator') return;
    21→
    22→  // Must be HTTPS
    23→  if (url.protocol !== 'https:') {
    24→    throw new Error('Endpoint must use HTTPS');
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  // Block private/internal IP ranges and metadata endpoints
    28→  const hostname = url.hostname;
    29→  if (
    30→    hostname === 'localhost' ||
    31→    hostname === '127.0.0.1' ||
    32→    hostname === '::1' ||
    33→    hostname.startsWith('10.') ||
    34→    hostname.startsWith('192.168.') ||
    35→    hostname === '169.254.169.254' ||
    36→    hostname.endsWith('.internal') ||
    37→    hostname.endsWith('.local') ||
    38→    hostname.startsWith('169.254.') ||
    39→    hostname.startsWith('fd') ||
    40→    hostname.startsWith('fe80')
    41→  ) {
    42→    throw new Error('Endpoint cannot target private/internal addresses');
    43→  }
    44→
    45→  // Block 172.16.0.0/12 range
    46→  if (hostname.startsWith('172.')) {
    47→    const second = parseInt(hostname.split('.')[1], 10);
    48→    if (second >= 16 && second <= 31) {
    49→      throw new Error('Endpoint cannot target private/internal addresses');
    50→    }
    51→  }
    52→}
    53→
    54→/**
    55→ * DNS rebinding protection: resolve hostname and verify IP is not private.
    56→ * Prevents attacker.com -> 169.254.169.254 attacks.
    57→ */
    58→export async function validateResolvedIp(hostname: string): Promise<void> {
    59→  const result = await lookup(hostname);
    60→  const ip = result.address;
    61→  if (
    62→    ip.startsWith('10.') ||
    63→    ip.startsWith('192.168.') ||
    64→    ip.startsWith('127.') ||
    65→    ip === '::1' ||
    66→    ip.startsWith('169.254.') ||
    67→    ip.startsWith('fd') ||
    68→    ip.startsWith('fe80')
    69→  ) {
    70→    throw new Error('Endpoint DNS resolves to private address');
    71→  }
    72→  if (ip.startsWith('172.')) {
    73→    const second = parseInt(ip.split('.')[1], 10);
    74→    if (second >= 16 && second <= 31) {
    75→      throw new Error('Endpoint DNS resolves to private address');
    76→    }
    77→  }
    78→}
    79→

    54→   * @throws ConflictException if vault already exists for user
    55→   */
    56→  async initializeVault(userId: string, dto: InitVaultDto): Promise<VaultResponseDto> {
    57→    // Check if vault already exists
    58→    const existingVault = await this.vaultRepository.findOne({
    59→      where: { ownerId: userId },
    60→    });
    61→
    62→    if (existingVault) {
    63→      throw new ConflictException('Vault already exists for this user');
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    // Decode hex strings to buffers
    67→    const vault = this.vaultRepository.create({
    68→      ownerId: userId,
    69→      ownerPublicKey: Buffer.from(dto.ownerPublicKey, 'hex'),
    70→      rootIpnsName: dto.rootIpnsName,
    71→      initializedAt: null,
    72→    });
    73→
    74→    const savedVault = await this.vaultRepository.save(vault);
    75→
    76→    // Ensure root folder IPNS entry exists for publish tracking.
    77→    // The IPNS publish endpoint may have already created this row (clients
    78→    // publish the root folder IPNS record before calling /vault/init).
    79→    // Try insert first; on duplicate key, update isRoot instead.
    80→    try {
    81→      const rootFolderIpns = this.folderIpnsRepository.create({
    82→        userId,
    83→        ipnsName: dto.rootIpnsName,
    84→        latestCid: null,
    85→        sequenceNumber: '0',
    86→        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: null,
    87→        keyEpoch: null,
    88→        isRoot: true,
    89→      });
    90→      await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(rootFolderIpns);
    91→    } catch (error) {
    92→      // Row already exists (from IPNS publish endpoint) — mark as root
    93→      if ((error as { code?: string }).code === '23505') {
    94→        await this.folderIpnsRepository.update(
    95→          { userId, ipnsName: dto.rootIpnsName },
    96→          { isRoot: true }
    97→        );
    98→      } else {
    99→        throw error;
   100→      }
   101→    }
   102→
   103→    const teeKeys = await this.teeKeyStateService.getTeeKeysDto();
   104→    return this.toVaultResponse(savedVault, teeKeys);
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  /**
   108→   * Get vault for a user
   109→   *
   110→   * @throws NotFoundException if vault does not exist
   111→   */
   112→  async getVault(userId: string): Promise<VaultResponseDto> {
   113→    const vault = await this.vaultRepository.findOne({
   114→      where: { ownerId: userId },
   115→    });
   116→
   117→    if (!vault) {
   118→      throw new NotFoundException('Vault not found');
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    const teeKeys = await this.teeKeyStateService.getTeeKeysDto();
   122→    return this.toVaultResponse(vault, teeKeys);
   123→  }
   124→
   125→  /**
   126→   * Check if vault exists for user (returns null if not)
   127→   */
   128→  async findVault(userId: string): Promise<VaultResponseDto | null> {
   129→    const vault = await this.vaultRepository.findOne({
   130→      where: { ownerId: userId },
   131→    });
   132→
   133→    if (!vault) return null;
   134→
   135→    const teeKeys = await this.teeKeyStateService.getTeeKeysDto();
   136→    return this.toVaultResponse(vault, teeKeys);
   137→  }
   138→
   139→  /**
   140→   * Check if user is in BYO (Bring Your Own) IPFS mode.
   141→   * BYO users manage their own IPFS node; quota is advisory only.
   142→   */
   143→  async isUserByo(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   144→    const vault = await this.vaultRepository.findOne({
   145→      where: { ownerId: userId },
   146→      select: ['isByoUser'],
   147→    });
   148→    return vault?.isByoUser ?? false;
   149→  }
   150→
   151→  /**
   152→   * Set BYO status for a user's vault.
   153→   */
   154→  async setByoStatus(userId: string, isByo: boolean): Promise<void> {
   155→    await this.vaultRepository.update({ ownerId: userId }, { isByoUser: isByo });
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  /**
   159→   * Get current storage quota usage for a user.
   160→   * For BYO users, the advisory flag is set to true.
   161→   */
   162→  async getQuota(userId: string): Promise<QuotaResponseDto> {
   163→    const isByo = await this.isUserByo(userId);
   164→
   165→    const result = await this.pinnedCidRepository
   166→      .createQueryBuilder('pin')
   167→      .select('COALESCE(SUM(pin.size_bytes), 0)', 'total')
   168→      .where('pin.user_id = :userId', { userId })
   169→      .getRawOne<{ total: string }>();
   170→
   171→    const usedBytes = parseInt(result?.total ?? '0', 10);
   172→    const remainingBytes = Math.max(0, QUOTA_LIMIT_BYTES - usedBytes);
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→      usedBytes,
   176→      limitBytes: QUOTA_LIMIT_BYTES,
   177→      remainingBytes,
   178→      advisory: isByo,
   179→    };
   180→  }
   181→
   182→  /**
   183→   * Check if user has sufficient quota for additional storage.
   184→   * BYO users always pass (advisory only -- no enforcement).
   185→   *
   186→   * @returns true if BYO user OR (current usage + additionalBytes) <= quota limit
   187→   */
   188→  async checkQuota(userId: string, additionalBytes: number): Promise<boolean> {
   189→    const isByo = await this.isUserByo(userId);
   190→    if (isByo) return true; // Advisory only -- always allow
   191→
   192→    const quota = await this.getQuota(userId);
   193→    return quota.usedBytes + additionalBytes <= QUOTA_LIMIT_BYTES;
   194→  }
   195→
   196→  /**
   197→   * Record a pinned CID for quota tracking
   198→   * Uses upsert (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING) for idempotency
   199→   */
   200→  async recordPin(userId: string, cid: string, sizeBytes: number): Promise<void> {
   201→    await this.pinnedCidRepository
   202→      .createQueryBuilder()
   203→      .insert()
   204→      .into(PinnedCid)
   205→      .values({
   206→        userId,
   207→        cid,
   208→        sizeBytes: sizeBytes.toString(),
   209→      })
   210→      .orIgnore() // ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING for idempotency
   211→      .execute();
   212→  }
   213→
   214→  /**
   215→   * Remove a pinned CID record
   216→   * Idempotent - no error if CID not found
   217→   */
   218→  async recordUnpin(userId: string, cid: string): Promise<void> {
   219→    await this.pinnedCidRepository.delete({
   220→      userId,
   221→      cid,
   222→    });
   223→  }
   224→
   225→  /**
   226→   * Mark vault as initialized (first file uploaded)
   227→   */
   228→  async markInitialized(userId: string): Promise<void> {
   229→    await this.vaultRepository.update({ ownerId: userId }, { initializedAt: new Date() });
   230→  }
   231→
   232→  /**
   233→   * Get export data for independent recovery.
   234→   * Returns the minimal set of fields needed to reconstruct the vault:
   235→   * root IPNS name + encrypted root keys + derivation hints.
   236→   */
   237→  async getExportData(userId: string): Promise<VaultExportDto> {
   238→    const vault = await this.vaultRepository.findOne({
   239→      where: { ownerId: userId },
   240→    });
   241→
   242→    if (!vault) {
   243→      throw new NotFoundException('Vault not found');
   244→    }
   245→
   246→    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
   247→      where: { id: userId },
   248→    });
   249→
   250→    return {
   251→      format: 'cipherbox-vault-export',
   252→      version: '1.0',
   253→      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   254→      rootIpnsName: vault.rootIpnsName,
   255→      derivationMethod: user ? 'web3auth' : null,
   256→    };
   257→  }
   258→
   259→  /**
   260→   * Convert Vault entity to response DTO with hex-encoded fields
   261→   */
   262→  private toVaultResponse(vault: Vault, teeKeys: TeeKeysDto | null = null): VaultResponseDto {
   263→    return {
   264→      id: vault.id,
   265→      ownerPublicKey: vault.ownerPublicKey.toString('hex'),
   266→      rootIpnsName: vault.rootIpnsName,
   267→      createdAt: vault.createdAt,
   268→      initializedAt: vault.initializedAt,
   269→      teeKeys,
   270→    };
   271→  }
   272→}
   273→
```

     1→import {
     2→  Injectable,
     3→  Logger,
     4→  ConflictException,
     5→  NotFoundException,
     6→  ForbiddenException,
     7→} from '@nestjs/common';
     8→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     9→import { InjectQueue } from '@nestjs/bullmq';
    10→import { Repository, In } from 'typeorm';
    11→import { Queue } from 'bullmq';
    12→import { PinMigration, MigrationStatus } from './migration.entity';
    13→import { PinnedCid } from '../vault/entities/pinned-cid.entity';
    14→import { StartMigrationDto } from './dto/start-migration.dto';
    15→import { MigrationStatusDto } from './dto/migration-status.dto';
    16→
    17→@Injectable()
    18→export class MigrationService {
    19→  private readonly logger = new Logger(MigrationService.name);
    20→
    21→  constructor(
    22→    @InjectRepository(PinMigration)
    23→    private readonly migrationRepo: Repository<PinMigration>,
    24→    @InjectRepository(PinnedCid)
    25→    private readonly pinnedCidRepo: Repository<PinnedCid>,
    26→    @InjectQueue('pin-migration')
    27→    private readonly migrationQueue: Queue
    28→  ) {}
    29→
    30→  /**
    31→   * Start a new pin migration for a user.
    32→   * Prevents concurrent migrations (only one active migration at a time).
    33→   */
    34→  async startMigration(userId: string, dto: StartMigrationDto): Promise<string> {
    35→    // Check for existing active migration
    36→    const activeMigration = await this.migrationRepo.findOne({
    37→      where: {
    38→        userId,
    39→        status: In(['pending', 'running', 'paused'] as MigrationStatus[]),
    40→      },
    41→    });
    42→
    43→    if (activeMigration) {
    44→      throw new ConflictException(
    45→        'An active migration already exists. Complete or cancel it before starting a new one.'
    46→      );
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    // Count pinned CIDs for this user
    50→    const pinnedCids = await this.pinnedCidRepo.find({ where: { userId } });
    51→    const totalCids = pinnedCids.length;
    52→
    53→    // Create migration entity
    54→    const migration = this.migrationRepo.create({
    55→      userId,
    56→      status: 'pending' as MigrationStatus,
    57→      totalCids,
    58→      migratedCids: 0,
    59→      failedCids: 0,
    60→      sourceConfigEncrypted: dto.sourceConfigEncrypted,
    61→      destConfigEncrypted: dto.destConfigEncrypted,
    62→      failedCidList: null,
    63→      completedAt: null,
    64→    });
    65→
    66→    const saved = await this.migrationRepo.save(migration);
    67→
    68→    // Enqueue BullMQ job
    69→    await this.migrationQueue.add('pin-migration', { migrationId: saved.id });
    70→
    71→    this.logger.log(`Migration ${saved.id} started for user ${userId} with ${totalCids} CIDs`);
    72→
    73→    return saved.id;
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  /**
    77→   * Get the latest migration status for a user.
    78→   */
    79→  async getStatus(userId: string): Promise<MigrationStatusDto | null> {
    80→    const migration = await this.migrationRepo.findOne({
    81→      where: { userId },
    82→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
    83→    });
    84→
    85→    if (!migration) {
    86→      return null;
    87→    }
    88→
    89→    return {
    90→      id: migration.id,
    91→      status: migration.status,
    92→      totalCids: migration.totalCids,
    93→      migratedCids: migration.migratedCids,
    94→      failedCids: migration.failedCids,
    95→      createdAt: migration.createdAt.toISOString(),
    96→      completedAt: migration.completedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    97→    };
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  /**
   101→   * Pause an active migration.
   102→   * The BullMQ processor checks status before processing each batch.
   103→   */
   104→  async pauseMigration(userId: string, migrationId: string): Promise<void> {
   105→    const migration = await this.findUserMigration(userId, migrationId);
   106→
   107→    if (migration.status !== 'running' && migration.status !== 'pending') {
   108→      throw new ConflictException(`Cannot pause migration in '${migration.status}' status`);
   109→    }
   110→
   111→    await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, {
   112→      status: 'paused' as MigrationStatus,
   113→    });
   114→
   115→    this.logger.log(`Migration ${migrationId} paused`);
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  /**
   119→   * Resume a paused migration.
   120→   */
   121→  async resumeMigration(userId: string, migrationId: string): Promise<void> {
   122→    const migration = await this.findUserMigration(userId, migrationId);
   123→
   124→    if (migration.status !== 'paused') {
   125→      throw new ConflictException(`Cannot resume migration in '${migration.status}' status`);
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, {
   129→      status: 'running' as MigrationStatus,
   130→    });
   131→
   132→    // Re-enqueue job to continue processing
   133→    await this.migrationQueue.add('pin-migration', { migrationId });
   134→
   135→    this.logger.log(`Migration ${migrationId} resumed`);
   136→  }
   137→
   138→  /**
   139→   * Cancel a migration.
   140→   */
   141→  async cancelMigration(userId: string, migrationId: string): Promise<void> {
   142→    const migration = await this.findUserMigration(userId, migrationId);
   143→
   144→    if (migration.status === 'completed' || migration.status === 'cancelled') {
   145→      throw new ConflictException(`Cannot cancel migration in '${migration.status}' status`);
   146→    }
   147→
   148→    await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, {
   149→      status: 'cancelled' as MigrationStatus,
   150→    });
   151→
   152→    this.logger.log(`Migration ${migrationId} cancelled`);
   153→  }
   154→
   155→  /**
   156→   * Update migration progress counters.
   157→   * Called by the BullMQ processor after each batch.
   158→   */
   159→  async updateProgress(
   160→    migrationId: string,
   161→    migratedDelta: number,
   162→    failedDelta: number,
   163→    failedCids?: string[]
   164→  ): Promise<void> {
   165→    const migration = await this.migrationRepo.findOneOrFail({
   166→      where: { id: migrationId },
   167→    });
   168→
   169→    migration.migratedCids += migratedDelta;
   170→    migration.failedCids += failedDelta;
   171→
   172→    if (failedCids && failedCids.length > 0) {
   173→      const existingList = migration.failedCidList ? migration.failedCidList.split(',') : [];
   174→      existingList.push(...failedCids);
   175→      migration.failedCidList = existingList.join(',');
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    await this.migrationRepo.save(migration);
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  /**
   182→   * Find a migration belonging to a specific user.
   183→   * Throws NotFoundException if not found, ForbiddenException if wrong user.
   184→   */
   185→  private async findUserMigration(userId: string, migrationId: string): Promise<PinMigration> {
   186→    const migration = await this.migrationRepo.findOne({
   187→      where: { id: migrationId },
   188→    });
   189→
   190→    if (!migration) {
   191→      throw new NotFoundException(`Migration ${migrationId} not found`);
   192→    }
   193→
   194→    if (migration.userId !== userId) {
   195→      throw new ForbiddenException('Migration does not belong to this user');
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    return migration;
   199→  }
   200→}
   201→

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2→import { migrationApi, type MigrationStatus } from '../../lib/api/migration';
     3→
     4→/**
     5→ * Migration progress UI for the STORAGE tab.
     6→ *
     7→ * Polls migration status every 5 seconds while a migration is active.
     8→ * Shows progress bar with migrated/total count, failed count in error color,
     9→ * and pause/resume/cancel controls with cancel confirmation dialog.
    10→ */
    11→export function MigrationProgress() {
    12→  const [migration, setMigration] = useState<MigrationStatus | null>(null);
    13→  const [showCancelConfirm, setShowCancelConfirm] = useState(false);
    14→  const pollRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
    15→
    16→  const fetchStatus = useCallback(async () => {
    17→    const status = await migrationApi.getStatus();
    18→    setMigration(status);
    19→    // Stop polling when terminal state reached
    20→    if (status && ['completed', 'failed', 'cancelled'].includes(status.status)) {
    21→      if (pollRef.current) {
    22→        clearInterval(pollRef.current);
    23→        pollRef.current = null;
    24→      }
    25→    }
    26→  }, []);
    27→
    28→  // Poll every 5 seconds when migration is active
    29→  useEffect(() => {
    30→    fetchStatus();
    31→    pollRef.current = setInterval(fetchStatus, 5000);
    32→    return () => {
    33→      if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current);
    34→    };
    35→  }, [fetchStatus]);
    36→
    37→  if (!migration || migration.status === 'cancelled') return null;
    38→
    39→  const percentage =
    40→    migration.totalCids > 0 ? (migration.migratedCids / migration.totalCids) * 100 : 0;
    41→
    42→  const isActive = migration.status === 'running' || migration.status === 'pending';
    43→  const isPaused = migration.status === 'paused';
    44→  const isComplete = migration.status === 'completed';
    45→
    46→  const handlePause = async () => {
    47→    await migrationApi.pause(migration.id);
    48→    fetchStatus();
    49→  };
    50→
    51→  const handleResume = async () => {
    52→    await migrationApi.resume(migration.id);
    53→    fetchStatus();
    54→  };
    55→
    56→  const handleCancel = async () => {
    57→    await migrationApi.cancel(migration.id);
    58→    setShowCancelConfirm(false);
    59→    fetchStatus();
    60→  };
    61→
    62→  return (
    63→    <div className="migration-progress">
    64→      <h3 className="settings-section-heading">{'// pin migration'}</h3>
    65→      <p className="settings-section-description">
    66→        {
    67→          'migrate existing pins between providers. data is transferred via tee -- your encryption keys are never exposed.'
    68→        }
    69→      </p>
    70→
    71→      {/* Progress bar */}
    72→      <div
    73→        className="migration-progress-bar"
    74→        role="progressbar"
    75→        aria-valuenow={migration.migratedCids}
    76→        aria-valuemin={0}
    77→        aria-valuemax={migration.totalCids}
    78→        aria-label="Pin migration progress"
    79→      >
    80→        <div
    81→          className="migration-progress-fill"
    82→          style={{ width: `${Math.min(percentage, 100)}%` }}
    83→        />
    84→      </div>
    85→
    86→      {/* Progress text */}
    87→      {isComplete ? (
    88→        <p className="migration-progress-text migration-complete">
    89→          {`> migration complete. ${migration.totalCids} pins transferred.`}
    90→        </p>
    91→      ) : (
    92→        <p className="migration-progress-text">
    93→          {`migrating: ${migration.migratedCids}/${migration.totalCids} pins`}
    94→        </p>
    95→      )}
    96→
    97→      {/* Failed count */}
    98→      {migration.failedCids > 0 && (
    99→        <p className="migration-failed-text">{`${migration.failedCids} pins failed`}</p>
   100→      )}
   101→
   102→      {/* Controls */}
   103→      {(isActive || isPaused) && (
   104→        <div className="migration-controls">
   105→          {isActive && (
   106→            <button type="button" className="storage-discard-btn" onClick={handlePause}>
   107→              {'[--pause migration]'}
   108→            </button>
   109→          )}
   110→          {isPaused && (
   111→            <button type="button" className="storage-discard-btn" onClick={handleResume}>
   112→              {'[--resume migration]'}
   113→            </button>
   114→          )}
   115→
   116→          {showCancelConfirm ? (
   117→            <div className="migration-cancel-confirm">
   118→              <p className="migration-cancel-text">
   119→                {`cancel migration: ${migration.migratedCids}/${migration.totalCids} pins transferred. pins already migrated will remain on the new provider. cancel?`}
   120→              </p>
   121→              <button type="button" className="storage-discard-btn" onClick={handleCancel}>
   122→                {'[--confirm cancel]'}
   123→              </button>
   124→              <button
   125→                type="button"
   126→                className="storage-discard-btn"
   127→                onClick={() => setShowCancelConfirm(false)}
   128→              >
   129→                {'[--keep migrating]'}
   130→              </button>
   131→            </div>
   132→          ) : (
   133→            <button
   134→              type="button"
   135→              className="storage-discard-btn"
   136→              onClick={() => setShowCancelConfirm(true)}
   137→            >
   138→              {'[--cancel migration]'}
   139→            </button>
   140→          )}
   141→        </div>
   142→      )}
   143→    </div>
   144→  );
   145→}
   146→

     1→/**
     2→ * Migration Worker Service
     3→ *
     4→ * Core logic for migrating encrypted pins between IPFS providers.
     5→ * Runs inside TEE: decrypts provider credentials in-enclave, fetches
     6→ * encrypted blobs from source, pins to destination, verifies CID integrity.
     7→ *
     8→ * SECURITY:
     9→ * - Auth tokens processed as Uint8Array and zeroed with .fill(0) in finally block
    10→ * - Endpoint URLs validated against SSRF (HTTPS-only, no private IPs, DNS rebinding check)
    11→ * - No plaintext content access (opaque encrypted ciphertext only)
    12→ */
    13→
    14→import { decrypt } from 'eciesjs';
    15→import { getKeypair } from './tee-keys.js';
    16→import { validateEndpointUrl, validateResolvedIp } from './ssrf-validation.js';
    17→
    18→export type ProviderConfig = {
    19→  endpoint: string;
    20→  authTokenBytes: Uint8Array; // SECURITY: Uint8Array for proper zeroing, NOT string
    21→  protocol: 'psa' | 'kubo' | 'cipherbox';
    22→};
    23→
    24→export type MigrationBatchResult = {
    25→  succeeded: string[];
    26→  failed: string[];
    27→};
    28→
    29→// SSRF validation imported from shared ssrf-validation module
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * Decrypt an ECIES-encrypted provider config using TEE's current epoch key.
    33→ * Returns raw bytes for parsing.
    34→ */
    35→async function decryptEcies(encryptedHex: string, teePrivateKey: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    36→  const ciphertext = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(encryptedHex, 'hex'));
    37→  return new Uint8Array(decrypt(teePrivateKey, ciphertext));
    38→}
    39→
    40→/**
    41→ * Parse decrypted config bytes into ProviderConfig.
    42→ * SECURITY: authToken stays as Uint8Array for proper zeroing.
    43→ */
    44→function parseProviderConfig(decryptedBytes: Uint8Array): ProviderConfig {
    45→  const text = new TextDecoder().decode(decryptedBytes);
    46→  const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as {
    47→    endpoint: string;
    48→    authToken?: string;
    49→    protocol: 'psa' | 'kubo' | 'cipherbox';
    50→  };
    51→  return {
    52→    endpoint: parsed.endpoint,
    53→    authTokenBytes: new TextEncoder().encode(parsed.authToken ?? ''),
    54→    protocol: parsed.protocol,
    55→  };
    56→}
    57→
    58→/** Get auth token as string for HTTP header -- caller must zero authTokenBytes after use */
    59→function authTokenString(config: ProviderConfig): string {
    60→  return new TextDecoder().decode(config.authTokenBytes);
    61→}
    62→
    63→/**
    64→ * Migrate a batch of CIDs from source to destination provider.
    65→ * Runs inside TEE -- credentials are decrypted in-enclave only.
    66→ */
    67→export async function migrateBatch(
    68→  cids: string[],
    69→  sourceConfigEncrypted: string,
    70→  destConfigEncrypted: string,
    71→  currentEpoch: number
    72→): Promise<MigrationBatchResult> {
    73→  // 1. Get TEE private key for this epoch
    74→  const keypair = await getKeypair(currentEpoch);
    75→  const teePrivateKey = keypair.privateKey;
    76→
    77→  // 2. Decrypt provider configs in-enclave (returns Uint8Array)
    78→  const sourceConfigBytes = await decryptEcies(sourceConfigEncrypted, teePrivateKey);
    79→  const destConfigBytes = await decryptEcies(destConfigEncrypted, teePrivateKey);
    80→
    81→  // Zero TEE private key immediately after decryption
    82→  teePrivateKey.fill(0);
    83→
    84→  const sourceConfig = parseProviderConfig(sourceConfigBytes);
    85→  const destConfig = parseProviderConfig(destConfigBytes);
    86→
    87→  // 2b. SSRF validation on both endpoints (skipped in simulator mode)
    88→  if (sourceConfig.endpoint !== 'cipherbox') {
    89→    validateEndpointUrl(sourceConfig.endpoint);
    90→    if (process.env.TEE_MODE !== 'simulator') {
    91→      await validateResolvedIp(new URL(sourceConfig.endpoint).hostname);
    92→    }
    93→  }
    94→  if (destConfig.endpoint !== 'cipherbox') {
    95→    validateEndpointUrl(destConfig.endpoint);
    96→    if (process.env.TEE_MODE !== 'simulator') {
    97→      await validateResolvedIp(new URL(destConfig.endpoint).hostname);
    98→    }
    99→  }
   100→
   101→  const succeeded: string[] = [];
   102→  const failed: string[] = [];
   103→
   104→  try {
   105→    for (const cid of cids) {
   106→      try {
   107→        // 3. Fetch encrypted blob from source
   108→        const data = await fetchFromProvider(cid, sourceConfig);
   109→
   110→        // 4. Pin to destination
   111→        const destCid = await pinToProvider(data, cid, destConfig);
   112→
   113→        // 5. Verify CID match (content integrity)
   114→        if (destCid !== cid) {
   115→          throw new Error(`CID mismatch: expected ${cid}, got ${destCid}`);
   116→        }
   117→
   118→        succeeded.push(cid);
   119→
   120→        // Best-effort source unpin after verified transfer
   121→        // Failure here is non-fatal -- the CID is already on the destination
   122→        try {
   123→          if (sourceConfig.protocol !== 'cipherbox') {
   124→            await unpinFromProvider(cid, sourceConfig);
   125→          }
   126→        } catch {
   127→          // Source unpin failure is non-fatal; CID is safely on destination
   128→        }
   129→      } catch {
   130→        failed.push(cid);
   131→      }
   132→    }
   133→  } finally {
   134→    // 6. Zero credentials from memory (Uint8Array.fill(0) -- same pattern as republish.ts)
   135→    sourceConfig.authTokenBytes.fill(0);
   136→    destConfig.authTokenBytes.fill(0);
   137→    sourceConfigBytes.fill(0);
   138→    destConfigBytes.fill(0);
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  return { succeeded, failed };
   142→}
   143→
   144→/**
   145→ * Unpin a CID from a provider after verified migration transfer.
   146→ * Supports Kubo (POST /pin/rm) and PSA (list by CID + DELETE) protocols.
   147→ * Best-effort: failures are non-fatal since the CID is already on the destination.
   148→ */
   149→async function unpinFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<void> {
   150→  const token = authTokenString(config);
   151→
   152→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   153→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   154→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   155→    const response = await fetch(
   156→      `${config.endpoint}/api/v0/pin/rm?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   157→      {
   158→        method: 'POST',
   159→        headers,
   160→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   161→      }
   162→    );
   163→    if (!response.ok) {
   164→      const errorText = await response.text();
   165→      // "not pinned" means already unpinned -- treat as success (idempotent)
   166→      if (errorText.toLowerCase().includes('not pinned')) return;
   167→      throw new Error(`Unpin from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   168→    }
   169→    return;
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  if (config.protocol === 'psa') {
   173→    // PSA requires finding the requestid first, then deleting
   174→    const listResponse = await fetch(
   175→      `${config.endpoint}/pins?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}&status=pinned,pinning,queued`,
   176→      {
   177→        method: 'GET',
   178→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   179→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   180→      }
   181→    );
   182→    if (!listResponse.ok) return; // Best-effort: if list fails, skip unpin
   183→    const listResult = (await listResponse.json()) as { results: Array<{ requestid: string }> };
   184→    for (const pin of listResult.results) {
   185→      await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins/${encodeURIComponent(pin.requestid)}`, {
   186→        method: 'DELETE',
   187→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   188→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   189→      });
   190→    }
   191→    return;
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  // 'cipherbox' protocol: unpin via CipherBox API -- not handled in TEE worker.
   195→  // CipherBox unpins are handled by the API-side MigrationProcessor.
   196→}
   197→
   198→async function fetchFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   199→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   200→    const token = authTokenString(config);
   201→    const headers: Record<string, string> = token ? { Authorization: `Basic ${token}` } : {};
   202→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/cat?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   203→      method: 'POST',
   204→      headers,
   205→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   206→    });
   207→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   208→    return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   209→  }
   210→
   211→  // CipherBox or PSA: use IPFS gateway to fetch content
   212→  const GATEWAY_URL = process.env.IPFS_GATEWAY_URL || 'https://ipfs.io';
   213→  const response = await fetch(`${GATEWAY_URL}/ipfs/${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   214→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   215→  });
   216→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from gateway failed: ${response.status}`);
   217→  return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   218→}
   219→
   220→async function pinToProvider(
   221→  data: Uint8Array,
   222→  expectedCid: string,
   223→  config: ProviderConfig
   224→): Promise<string> {
   225→  const token = authTokenString(config);
   226→
   227→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   228→    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   229→    const blob = new Blob([data as any]);
   230→    const formData = new FormData();
   231→    formData.append('file', blob);
   232→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   233→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   234→
   235→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/add?pin=true&cid-version=1`, {
   236→      method: 'POST',
   237→      body: formData,
   238→      headers,
   239→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   240→    });
   241→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Pin to Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   242→    const result = (await response.json()) as { Hash: string };
   243→    return result.Hash;
   244→  }
   245→
   246→  // PSA: pin by CID reference (data must already exist on IPFS network)
   247→  const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins`, {
   248→    method: 'POST',
   249→    headers: {
   250→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   251→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   252→    },
   253→    body: JSON.stringify({
   254→      cid: expectedCid,
   255→      name: `migration-${Date.now()}`,
   256→    }),
   257→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   258→  });
   259→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`PSA pin failed: ${response.status}`);
   260→  const result = (await response.json()) as { pin: { cid: string } };
   261→  return result.pin.cid;
   262→}
   263→
   264→// NOTE: No zeroString function -- JS strings are immutable and cannot be zeroed.
   265→// All credentials are processed as Uint8Array throughout and zeroed with .fill(0)
   266→// in the finally block of migrateBatch(). This follows the existing pattern from
   267→// tee-worker/src/routes/republish.ts:86 (ipnsPrivateKey.fill(0)).
   268→

address');\n    }\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "import { lookup } from 'node:dns/promises';\n\n/**\n * Check if an address (hostname or resolved IP) is in a private/internal range.\n */\nfunction isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {\n  return (\n    addr === 'localhost' ||\n    addr === '127.0.0.1' ||\n    addr === '::1' ||\n    addr.startsWith('10.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('192.168.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('127.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('169.254.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('fd') ||\n    addr.startsWith('fe80') ||\n    addr.endsWith('.internal') ||\n    addr.endsWith('.local') ||\n    is172Private(addr)\n  );\n}\n\nfunction is172Private(addr: string): boolean {\n  if (!addr.startsWith('172.')) return false;\n  const second = parseInt(addr.split('.')[1], 10);\n  return second >= 16 && second <= 31;\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate endpoint URL to prevent SSRF attacks.\n * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.\n */\nexport function validateEndpointUrl(endpoint: string): void {\n  const url = new URL(endpoint);\n\n  if (process.env.TEE_MODE === 'simulator') return;\n\n  if (url.protocol !== 'https:') {\n    throw new Error('Endpoint must use HTTPS');\n  }\n\n  if (isPrivateAddress(url.hostname)) {\n    throw new Error('Endpoint cannot target private/internal addresses');\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * DNS rebinding protection: resolve hostname and verify IP is not private.\n * Prevents attacker.com -> 169.254.169.254 attacks.\n */\nexport async function validateResolvedIp(hostname: string): Promise<void> {\n  const result = await lookup(hostname);\n  if (isPrivateAddress(result.address)) {\n    throw new Error('Endpoint DNS resolves to private address');\n  }\n}"
}

  "new_string": "  const TERMINAL_STATUSES = ['completed', 'failed', 'cancelled'];\n\n  const fetchStatus = useCallback(async () => {\n    const status = await migrationApi.getStatus();\n    // Only update state if values actually changed to avoid no-op re-renders\n    setMigration((prev) => {\n      if (!status && !prev) return prev;\n      if (\n        prev &&\n        status &&\n        prev.status === status.status &&\n        prev.migratedCids === status.migratedCids &&\n        prev.failedCids === status.failedCids\n      ) {\n        return prev;\n      }\n      return status;\n    });\n    // Stop polling when terminal state reached or no migration exists\n    if (!status || TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(status.status)) {\n      if (pollRef.current) {\n        clearInterval(pollRef.current);\n        pollRef.current = null;\n      }\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchStatus();\n    pollRef.current = setInterval(fetchStatus, 5000);\n    return () => {\n      if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current);\n    };\n  }, [fetchStatus]);"

   100→
   101→  const succeeded: string[] = [];
   102→  const failed: string[] = [];
   103→
   104→  // Decode auth tokens once for the entire batch instead of per-CID
   105→  const sourceToken = authTokenString(sourceConfig);
   106→  const destToken = authTokenString(destConfig);
   107→
   108→  try {
   109→    for (const cid of cids) {
   110→      try {
   111→        // 3. Fetch encrypted blob from source
   112→        const data = await fetchFromProvider(cid, sourceConfig);
   113→
   114→        // 4. Pin to destination
   115→        const destCid = await pinToProvider(data, cid, destConfig);
   116→
   117→        // 5. Verify CID match (content integrity)
   118→        if (destCid !== cid) {
   119→          throw new Error(`CID mismatch: expected ${cid}, got ${destCid}`);
   120→        }
   121→
   122→        succeeded.push(cid);
   123→
   124→        // Best-effort source unpin after verified transfer
   125→        // Failure here is non-fatal -- the CID is already on the destination
   126→        try {
   127→          if (sourceConfig.protocol !== 'cipherbox') {
   128→            await unpinFromProvider(cid, sourceConfig);
   129→          }
   130→        } catch {
   131→          // Source unpin failure is non-fatal; CID is safely on destination
   132→        }
   133→      } catch {
   134→        failed.push(cid);
   135→      }
   136→    }
   137→  } finally {
   138→    // 6. Zero credentials from memory (Uint8Array.fill(0) -- same pattern as republish.ts)
   139→    sourceConfig.authTokenBytes.fill(0);
   140→    destConfig.authTokenBytes.fill(0);
   141→    sourceConfigBytes.fill(0);
   142→    destConfigBytes.fill(0);
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  return { succeeded, failed };
   146→}
   147→
   148→/**
   149→ * Unpin a CID from a provider after verified migration transfer.
   150→ * Supports Kubo (POST /pin/rm) and PSA (list by CID + DELETE) protocols.
   151→ * Best-effort: failures are non-fatal since the CID is already on the destination.
   152→ */
   153→async function unpinFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<void> {
   154→  const token = authTokenString(config);
   155→
   156→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   157→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   158→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   159→    const response = await fetch(
   160→      `${config.endpoint}/api/v0/pin/rm?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   161→      {
   162→        method: 'POST',
   163→        headers,
   164→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   165→      }
   166→    );
   167→    if (!response.ok) {
   168→      const errorText = await response.text();
   169→      // "not pinned" means already unpinned -- treat as success (idempotent)
   170→      if (errorText.toLowerCase().includes('not pinned')) return;
   171→      throw new Error(`Unpin from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   172→    }
   173→    return;
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  if (config.protocol === 'psa') {
   177→    // PSA requires finding the requestid first, then deleting
   178→    const listResponse = await fetch(
   179→      `${config.endpoint}/pins?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}&status=pinned,pinning,queued`,
   180→      {
   181→        method: 'GET',
   182→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   183→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   184→      }
   185→    );
   186→    if (!listResponse.ok) return; // Best-effort: if list fails, skip unpin
   187→    const listResult = (await listResponse.json()) as { results: Array<{ requestid: string }> };
   188→    for (const pin of listResult.results) {
   189→      await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins/${encodeURIComponent(pin.requestid)}`, {
   190→        method: 'DELETE',
   191→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   192→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   193→      });
   194→    }
   195→    return;
   196→  }
   197→
   198→  // 'cipherbox' protocol: unpin via CipherBox API -- not handled in TEE worker.
   199→  // CipherBox unpins are handled by the API-side MigrationProcessor.
   200→}
   201→
   202→async function fetchFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   203→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   204→    const token = authTokenString(config);
   205→    const headers: Record<string, string> = token ? { Authorization: `Basic ${token}` } : {};
   206→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/cat?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   207→      method: 'POST',
   208→      headers,
   209→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   210→    });
   211→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   212→    return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   213→  }
   214→
   215→  // CipherBox or PSA: use IPFS gateway to fetch content
   216→  const GATEWAY_URL = process.env.IPFS_GATEWAY_URL || 'https://ipfs.io';
   217→  const response = await fetch(`${GATEWAY_URL}/ipfs/${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   218→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   219→  });
   220→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from gateway failed: ${response.status}`);
   221→  return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   222→}
   223→
   224→async function pinToProvider(
   225→  data: Uint8Array,
   226→  expectedCid: string,
   227→  config: ProviderConfig
   228→): Promise<string> {
   229→  const token = authTokenString(config);
   230→
   231→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   232→    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   233→    const blob = new Blob([data as any]);
   234→    const formData = new FormData();
   235→    formData.append('file', blob);
   236→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   237→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   238→
   239→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/add?pin=true&cid-version=1`, {
   240→      method: 'POST',
   241→      body: formData,
   242→      headers,
   243→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   244→    });
   245→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Pin to Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   246→    const result = (await response.json()) as { Hash: string };
   247→    return result.Hash;
   248→  }
   249→
   250→  // PSA: pin by CID reference (data must already exist on IPFS network)
   251→  const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins`, {
   252→    method: 'POST',
   253→    headers: {
   254→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   255→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   256→    },
   257→    body: JSON.stringify({
   258→      cid: expectedCid,
   259→      name: `migration-${Date.now()}`,
   260→    }),
   261→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   262→  });
   263→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`PSA pin failed: ${response.status}`);
   264→  const result = (await response.json()) as { pin: { cid: string } };
   265→  return result.pin.cid;
   266→}
   267→
   268→// NOTE: No zeroString function -- JS strings are immutable and cannot be zeroed.
   269→// All credentials are processed as Uint8Array throughout and zeroed with .fill(0)
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of what was fixed:

**Bug fix:**
- `ByoIpfsConfig.protocol` — added `'pinata'` to type union (was missing, breaking Pinata config round-trips)

**Deduplication:**
- `StorageQuota.tsx` — removed local `formatBytes`, imported from existing `utils/format.ts`
- `ssrf-validation.ts` — extracted `isPrivateAddress()` helper, eliminating copy-pasted IP range checks
- `useAuth.ts` — replaced magic string `'cipherbox-byo-ipns-name'` with local constant (matches StorageTab pattern)

**Efficiency:**
- `vault.service.ts` — `checkQuota` now queries usage directly instead of calling `getQuota` (which called `isUserByo` a second time)
- `migration.service.ts` — `startMigration` uses `.count()` instead of `.find()` to avoid loading all PinnedCid rows
- `migration-worker.ts` — `authTokenString` decoded once per batch instead of per-CID (avoids 3N TextDecoder allocations)
- `MigrationProgress.tsx` — stops polling when no migration exists; skips `setMigration` when values haven't changed

**Cleanup:**
- `StorageTab.tsx` — removed 11 numbered step comments that narrated obvious code

The security review is complete. Here is a summary of the findings:

## SECURITY REVIEW COMPLETE

**Files analyzed:** 28
**Crypto operations found:** 14
**Issues found:** 2 High, 3 Medium, 4 Low, 2 Informational

### High Priority Findings

1. **HIGH-01 -- SSRF Blocklist Gaps** (`tee-worker/src/services/ssrf-validation.ts:15-29`): The `isPrivateAddress` function is missing `0.0.0.0`, IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses (`::ffff:127.0.0.1`), CGN range (`100.64.0.0/10`), and `fc00::` unique-local addresses. An attacker could use these to reach cloud metadata services or internal endpoints from the TEE worker.

2. **HIGH-02 -- DNS Rebinding TOCTOU** (`tee-worker/src/routes/connection-test.ts:71-74`, `tee-worker/src/services/migration-worker.ts:87-99`): DNS is resolved twice -- once for validation, once for fetch. An attacker controlling DNS can return a public IP during validation and a private IP during the actual fetch, completely bypassing SSRF protection. Additionally, redirects are followed by default, providing another SSRF bypass vector.

### Medium Priority Findings

3. **MEDIUM-01** (`tee-worker/src/services/migration-worker.ts:59-61,105-106`): Auth tokens are converted from `Uint8Array` to immutable JS strings for HTTP headers and persist in the heap for the entire batch duration, defeating the zeroing strategy.

4. **MEDIUM-02** (`apps/api/src/migration/migration.controller.ts`): All five migration endpoints have no `@Throttle` decorator, unlike the connection-test endpoint (10/min) and register-cid endpoint (100/hour).

5. **MEDIUM-03** (`tee-worker/src/routes/migrate.ts:26-37`): No maximum CID batch size, no CID format validation, and no max length on encrypted config strings at either the TEE or API DTO level.

### What Went Well

- **ECIES credential flow** is correctly implemented end-to-end: encrypt in browser, opaque passthrough at API, decrypt only in TEE
- **Key derivation** for BYO config IPNS uses proper HKDF-SHA256 with unique domain separation (`cipherbox-byo-ipfs-config-v1`)
- **TEE auth middleware** uses `crypto.timingSafeEqual` with a length pre-check -- textbook constant-time comparison
- **ECIES implementation** validates curve points, uses generic error messages to prevent oracle attacks
- **No credentials logged** anywhere in the codebase
- **CID integrity verification** in migration -- destination CID must match source CID

### Report Location

## Top Issues

1. **HIGH: SSRF blocklist gaps** — missing `0.0.0.0`, IPv4-mapped IPv6 (`::ffff:127.0.0.1`), CGN range (`100.64.0.0/10`)
2. **HIGH: DNS rebinding TOCTOU** — DNS resolved twice (once for validation, once for fetch). Attacker-controlled DNS can bypass SSRF. Redirects followed by default.
3. **MEDIUM: Migration endpoints unthrottled** — no rate limiting on 5 migration endpoints
4. **MEDIUM: No batch size limit** — TEE `/migrate` accepts unbounded CID arrays
5. **MEDIUM: Auth token string persistence** — `authTokenString()` creates immutable JS strings that survive until GC

## Positive Findings

- ECIES credential flow correctly end-to-end
- HKDF key derivation with proper domain separation
- Constant-time auth comparison in TEE middleware
- No credentials logged anywhere
- CID integrity verification in migration

**Full report:** `.planning/security/REVIEW-2026-03-25-phase-21.md`
