```
     1→/**
     2→ * SSRF Protection Utilities
     3→ *
     4→ * Validates user-provided endpoint URLs to prevent SSRF attacks.
     5→ * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.
     6→ *
     7→ * Extracted from migration-worker.ts for reuse across TEE routes.
     8→ */
     9→
    10→import { lookup } from 'node:dns/promises';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Check if an address (hostname or resolved IP) is in a private/internal range.
    14→ */
    15→function isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {
    16→  return (
    17→    addr === 'localhost' ||
    18→    addr === '127.0.0.1' ||
    19→    addr === '::1' ||
    20→    addr.startsWith('10.') ||
    21→    addr.startsWith('192.168.') ||
    22→    addr.startsWith('127.') ||
    23→    addr.startsWith('169.254.') ||
    24→    addr.startsWith('fd') ||
    25→    addr.startsWith('fe80') ||
    26→    addr.endsWith('.internal') ||
    27→    addr.endsWith('.local') ||
    28→    is172Private(addr)
    29→  );
    30→}
    31→
    32→function is172Private(addr: string): boolean {
    33→  if (!addr.startsWith('172.')) return false;
    34→  const second = parseInt(addr.split('.')[1], 10);
    35→  return second >= 16 && second <= 31;
    36→}
    37→
    38→/**
    39→ * Validate endpoint URL to prevent SSRF attacks.
    40→ * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.
    41→ */
    42→export function validateEndpointUrl(endpoint: string): void {
    43→  const url = new URL(endpoint);
    44→
    45→  if (process.env.TEE_MODE === 'simulator') return;
    46→
    47→  if (url.protocol !== 'https:') {
    48→    throw new Error('Endpoint must use HTTPS');
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  if (isPrivateAddress(url.hostname)) {
    52→    throw new Error('Endpoint cannot target private/internal addresses');
    53→  }
    54→}
    55→
    56→/**
    57→ * DNS rebinding protection: resolve hostname and verify IP is not private.
    58→ * Prevents attacker.com -> 169.254.169.254 attacks.
    59→ */
    60→export async function validateResolvedIp(hostname: string): Promise<void> {
    61→  const result = await lookup(hostname);
    62→  if (isPrivateAddress(result.address)) {
    63→    throw new Error('Endpoint DNS resolves to private address');
    64→  }
    65→}
    66→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Connection Test Route
     3→ *
     4→ * POST /connection-test - Server-side IPFS endpoint connection test.
     5→ *
     6→ * Receives ECIES-encrypted provider config (endpoint + authToken),
     7→ * decrypts in-enclave, probes the endpoint for Kubo RPC or PSA protocol,
     8→ * and returns the result. Avoids browser CORS issues since the probe
     9→ * runs server-side.
    10→ *
    11→ * SECURITY:
    12→ * - Provider credentials decrypted only inside TEE
    13→ * - Auth token bytes zeroed after probing completes
    14→ * - SSRF protection on user-provided endpoint URLs
    15→ * - TEE private key zeroed immediately after decryption
    16→ */
    17→
    18→import { Router, type Request, type Response } from 'express';
    19→import { decrypt } from 'eciesjs';
    20→import { getKeypair } from '../services/tee-keys.js';
    21→import { validateEndpointUrl, validateResolvedIp } from '../services/ssrf-validation.js';
    22→
    23→/** Timeout for each connection probe (10 seconds) */
    24→const PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
    25→
    26→const router = Router();
    27→
    28→router.post('/connection-test', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    29→  const { encryptedConfig, epoch } = req.body as {
    30→    encryptedConfig?: string;
    31→    epoch?: number;
    32→  };
    33→
    34→  if (!encryptedConfig || epoch === undefined || epoch === null) {
    35→    res.status(400).json({
    36→      success: false,
    37→      latencyMs: 0,
    38→      error: 'Missing required fields: encryptedConfig, epoch',
    39→    });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  let configBytes: Uint8Array | null = null;
    44→  let tokenBytes: Uint8Array | null = null;
    45→
    46→  try {
    47→    // 1. Get TEE keypair for this epoch
    48→    const keypair = await getKeypair(epoch);
    49→
    50→    // 2. Decrypt ECIES-encrypted config in-enclave
    51→    const ciphertext = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(encryptedConfig, 'hex'));
    52→    configBytes = new Uint8Array(decrypt(keypair.privateKey, ciphertext));
    53→
    54→    // 3. Zero TEE private key immediately
    55→    keypair.privateKey.fill(0);
    56→
    57→    // 4. Parse config
    58→    const configText = new TextDecoder().decode(configBytes);
    59→    const { endpoint, authToken } = JSON.parse(configText) as {
    60→      endpoint: string;
    61→      authToken?: string;
    62→    };
    63→
    64→    // 5. Create Uint8Array copy of auth token for zeroing
    65→    tokenBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(authToken ?? '');
    66→
    67→    // 6. Normalize endpoint
    68→    const normalizedEndpoint = endpoint.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    69→
    70→    // 7. SSRF validation (skipped in simulator mode)
    71→    validateEndpointUrl(normalizedEndpoint);
    72→    if (process.env.TEE_MODE !== 'simulator') {
    73→      await validateResolvedIp(new URL(normalizedEndpoint).hostname);
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    // 8. Sequential probe: Kubo first, then PSA
    77→    const kuboResult = await probeKubo(normalizedEndpoint, authToken);
    78→    if (kuboResult) {
    79→      res.status(200).json(kuboResult);
    80→      return;
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    const psaResult = await probePsa(normalizedEndpoint, authToken);
    84→    if (psaResult) {
    85→      res.status(200).json(psaResult);
    86→      return;
    87→    }
    88→
    89→    // Neither protocol detected
    90→    res.status(200).json({
    91→      success: false,
    92→      latencyMs: 0,
    93→      error: 'could not detect ipfs protocol at this endpoint.',
    94→    });
    95→  } catch (err) {
    96→    console.error('Connection test failed:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error');
    97→    res.status(200).json({
    98→      success: false,
    99→      latencyMs: 0,
   100→      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Connection test failed',
   101→    });
   102→  } finally {
   103→    // Zero sensitive data
   104→    if (configBytes) configBytes.fill(0);
   105→    if (tokenBytes) tokenBytes.fill(0);
   106→  }
   107→});
   108→
   109→/** Probe Kubo RPC endpoint via POST /api/v0/id */
   110→async function probeKubo(
   111→  endpoint: string,
   112→  authToken?: string
   113→): Promise<{
   114→  success: boolean;
   115→  protocol?: string;
   116→  version?: string;
   117→  latencyMs: number;
   118→  error?: string;
   119→} | null> {
   120→  const url = `${endpoint}/api/v0/id`;
   121→  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   122→  if (authToken) {
   123→    headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${authToken}`;
   124→  }
   125→
   126→  const start = performance.now();
   127→
   128→  try {
   129→    const response = await fetch(url, {
   130→      method: 'POST',
   131→      headers,
   132→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   133→    });
   134→
   135→    const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   136→
   137→    if (response.ok) {
   138→      const data = (await response.json()) as { AgentVersion?: string };
   139→      return {
   140→        success: true,
   141→        protocol: 'kubo',
   142→        version: data.AgentVersion,
   143→        latencyMs,
   144→      };
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    // 401/403/422 means the endpoint exists and speaks Kubo, but auth failed
   148→    if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403 || response.status === 422) {
   149→      return {
   150→        success: false,
   151→        protocol: 'kubo',
   152→        latencyMs,
   153→        error: 'authentication failed. check your auth token.',
   154→      };
   155→    }
   156→
   157→    // Other non-200 -- not Kubo, try PSA
   158→    return null;
   159→  } catch (err) {
   160→    const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   161→
   162→    if (isTimeoutError(err)) {
   163→      return {
   164→        success: false,
   165→        latencyMs,
   166→        error: 'connection timed out after 10 seconds.',
   167→      };
   168→    }
   169→
   170→    // Other errors (DNS failure, network down) -- try PSA
   171→    return null;
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
   175→/** Probe PSA endpoint via GET /pins?limit=1 */
   176→async function probePsa(
   177→  endpoint: string,
   178→  authToken?: string
   179→): Promise<{ success: boolean; protocol?: string; latencyMs: number; error?: string } | null> {
   180→  const url = `${endpoint}/pins?limit=1`;
   181→  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   182→  if (authToken) {
   183→    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${authToken}`;
   184→  }
   185→
   186→  const start = performance.now();
   187→
   188→  try {
   189→    const response = await fetch(url, {
   190→      method: 'GET',
   191→      headers,
   192→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   193→    });
   194→
   195→    const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   196→
   197→    if (response.ok) {
   198→      return {
   199→        success: true,
   200→        protocol: 'psa',
   201→        latencyMs,
   202→      };
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403 || response.status === 422) {
   206→      return {
   207→        success: false,
   208→        protocol: 'psa',
   209→        latencyMs,
   210→        error: 'authentication failed. check your auth token.',
   211→      };
   212→    }
   213→
   214→    // Other non-200 status -- not recognizable as PSA
   215→    return null;
   216→  } catch (err) {
   217→    const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);
   218→
   219→    if (isTimeoutError(err)) {
   220→      return {
   221→        success: false,
   222→        latencyMs,
   223→        error: 'connection timed out after 10 seconds.',
   224→      };
   225→    }
   226→
   227→    // Other errors
   228→    return null;
   229→  }
   230→}
   231→
   232→/** Detect AbortSignal timeout errors (Node.js style) */
   233→function isTimeoutError(err: unknown): boolean {
   234→  if (err instanceof Error && err.name === 'AbortError') return true;
   235→  // Node.js 20+ uses TimeoutError
   236→  if (err instanceof Error && err.name === 'TimeoutError') return true;
   237→  return false;
   238→}
   239→
   240→export default router;
   241→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Migration Route
     3→ *
     4→ * POST /migrate - Batch CID migration endpoint.
     5→ *
     6→ * Receives ECIES-encrypted provider configs and a list of CIDs to migrate.
     7→ * Decrypts configs in-enclave, fetches encrypted blobs from source provider,
     8→ * pins to destination provider, and verifies CID integrity.
     9→ *
    10→ * SECURITY:
    11→ * - Provider credentials decrypted only inside TEE
    12→ * - Auth tokens zeroed after migration batch completes
    13→ * - SSRF protection on user-provided endpoint URLs
    14→ * - No plaintext content access (opaque encrypted ciphertext)
    15→ */
    16→
    17→import { Router, type Request, type Response } from 'express';
    18→import { migrateBatch } from '../services/migration-worker.js';
    19→
    20→const router = Router();
    21→
    22→/** Current TEE epoch -- in production this would come from tee-keys state */
    23→const TEE_EPOCH = parseInt(process.env.TEE_CURRENT_EPOCH || '1', 10);
    24→
    25→router.post('/migrate', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    26→  const { cids, sourceConfigEncrypted, destConfigEncrypted } = req.body as {
    27→    cids?: string[];
    28→    sourceConfigEncrypted?: string;
    29→    destConfigEncrypted?: string;
    30→  };
    31→
    32→  if (!cids || !Array.isArray(cids) || !sourceConfigEncrypted || !destConfigEncrypted) {
    33→    res.status(400).json({
    34→      error: 'Missing required fields: cids, sourceConfigEncrypted, destConfigEncrypted',
    35→    });
    36→    return;
    37→  }
    38→
    39→  try {
    40→    const result = await migrateBatch(cids, sourceConfigEncrypted, destConfigEncrypted, TEE_EPOCH);
    41→
    42→    // Log migration summary (NEVER log credentials or config contents)
    43→    console.log(
    44→      `Migration batch: ${cids.length} CIDs, ${result.succeeded.length} succeeded, ${result.failed.length} failed`
    45→    );
    46→
    47→    res.status(200).json(result);
    48→  } catch (err) {
    49→    console.error('Migration batch failed:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error');
    50→    res.status(500).json({
    51→      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Migration failed',
    52→    });
    53→  }
    54→});
    55→
    56→export default router;
    57→
```

```json
  "old_string": "/**\n * Check if an address (hostname or resolved IP) is in a private/internal range.\n */\nfunction isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {\n  return (\n    addr === 'localhost' ||\n    addr === '127.0.0.1' ||\n    addr === '::1' ||\n    addr.startsWith('10.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('192.168.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('127.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('169.254.') ||\n    addr.startsWith('fd') ||\n    addr.startsWith('fe80') ||\n    addr.endsWith('.internal') ||\n    addr.endsWith('.local') ||\n    is172Private(addr)\n  );\n}\n\nfunction is172Private(addr: string): boolean {\n  if (!addr.startsWith('172.')) return false;\n  const second = parseInt(addr.split('.')[1], 10);\n  return second >= 16 && second <= 31;\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Check if an address (hostname or resolved IP) is in a private/internal range.\n *\n * Covers: RFC 1918, loopback, link-local, CGN (RFC 6598), unique-local IPv6,\n * IPv4-mapped IPv6, metadata endpoints, and .internal/.local suffixes.\n */\nfunction isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {\n  // Normalize: strip IPv4-mapped IPv6 prefix (::ffff:127.0.0.1 → 127.0.0.1)\n  const normalized = addr.startsWith('::ffff:') ? addr.slice(7) : addr;\n\n  return (\n    normalized === 'localhost' ||\n    normalized === '0.0.0.0' ||\n    normalized === '127.0.0.1' ||\n    normalized === '::1' ||\n    normalized === '::' ||\n    normalized.startsWith('10.') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('192.168.') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('127.') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('169.254.') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('fd') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('fc') ||\n    normalized.startsWith('fe80') ||\n    normalized.endsWith('.internal') ||\n    normalized.endsWith('.local') ||\n    is172Private(normalized) ||\n    isCgnRange(normalized)\n  );\n}\n\n/** RFC 1918: 172.16.0.0/12 */\nfunction is172Private(addr: string): boolean {\n  if (!addr.startsWith('172.')) return false;\n  const second = parseInt(addr.split('.')[1], 10);\n  return second >= 16 && second <= 31;\n}\n\n/** RFC 6598: Carrier-Grade NAT 100.64.0.0/10 */\nfunction isCgnRange(addr: string): boolean {\n  if (!addr.startsWith('100.')) return false;\n  const second = parseInt(addr.split('.')[1], 10);\n  return second >= 64 && second <= 127;\n}"
}
```

```
    56→/**
    57→ * Validate endpoint URL to prevent SSRF attacks.
    58→ * TEE worker fetches from user-provided URLs -- must block internal/metadata endpoints.
    59→ */
    60→export function validateEndpointUrl(endpoint: string): void {
    61→  const url = new URL(endpoint);
    62→
    63→  if (process.env.TEE_MODE === 'simulator') return;
    64→
    65→  if (url.protocol !== 'https:') {
    66→    throw new Error('Endpoint must use HTTPS');

    70→    throw new Error('Endpoint cannot target private/internal addresses');
    71→  }
    72→}
```

```json
  "new_string": "/**\n * DNS rebinding protection: resolve hostname and verify IP is not private.\n * Prevents attacker.com -> 169.254.169.254 attacks.\n */\nexport async function validateResolvedIp(hostname: string): Promise<void> {\n  const result = await lookup(hostname);\n  if (isPrivateAddress(result.address)) {\n    throw new Error('Endpoint DNS resolves to private address');\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Shared fetch wrapper that disables redirects to prevent SSRF via redirect.\n * An attacker's server could 302 to http://169.254.169.254/... after passing\n * the initial URL validation.\n */\nexport async function ssrfSafeFetch(\n  url: string,\n  init?: RequestInit\n): Promise<Response> {\n  return fetch(url, { ...init, redirect: 'error' });\n}"
}
```

```
   148→/**
   149→ * Unpin a CID from a provider after verified migration transfer.
   150→ * Supports Kubo (POST /pin/rm) and PSA (list by CID + DELETE) protocols.
   151→ * Best-effort: failures are non-fatal since the CID is already on the destination.
   152→ */
   153→async function unpinFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig, token: string): Promise<void> {
   154→
   155→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   156→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   157→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   158→    const response = await fetch(
   159→      `${config.endpoint}/api/v0/pin/rm?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`,
   160→      {
   161→        method: 'POST',
   162→        headers,
   163→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   164→      }
   165→    );
   166→    if (!response.ok) {
   167→      const errorText = await response.text();
   168→      // "not pinned" means already unpinned -- treat as success (idempotent)
   169→      if (errorText.toLowerCase().includes('not pinned')) return;
   170→      throw new Error(`Unpin from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   171→    }
   172→    return;
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  if (config.protocol === 'psa') {
   176→    // PSA requires finding the requestid first, then deleting
   177→    const listResponse = await fetch(
   178→      `${config.endpoint}/pins?cid=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}&status=pinned,pinning,queued`,
   179→      {
   180→        method: 'GET',
   181→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   182→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   183→      }
   184→    );
   185→    if (!listResponse.ok) return; // Best-effort: if list fails, skip unpin
   186→    const listResult = (await listResponse.json()) as { results: Array<{ requestid: string }> };
   187→    for (const pin of listResult.results) {
   188→      await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins/${encodeURIComponent(pin.requestid)}`, {
   189→        method: 'DELETE',
   190→        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
   191→        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   192→      });
   193→    }
   194→    return;
   195→  }
   196→
   197→  // 'cipherbox' protocol: unpin via CipherBox API -- not handled in TEE worker.
   198→  // CipherBox unpins are handled by the API-side MigrationProcessor.
   199→}
   200→
   201→async function fetchFromProvider(cid: string, config: ProviderConfig, token: string): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   202→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   203→    const headers: Record<string, string> = token ? { Authorization: `Basic ${token}` } : {};
   204→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/cat?arg=${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   205→      method: 'POST',
   206→      headers,
   207→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   208→    });
   209→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   210→    return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   211→  }
   212→
   213→  // CipherBox or PSA: use IPFS gateway to fetch content
   214→  const GATEWAY_URL = process.env.IPFS_GATEWAY_URL || 'https://ipfs.io';
   215→  const response = await fetch(`${GATEWAY_URL}/ipfs/${encodeURIComponent(cid)}`, {
   216→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   217→  });
   218→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch from gateway failed: ${response.status}`);
   219→  return new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer());
   220→}
   221→
   222→async function pinToProvider(
   223→  data: Uint8Array,
   224→  expectedCid: string,
   225→  config: ProviderConfig,
   226→  token: string
   227→): Promise<string> {
   228→
   229→  if (config.protocol === 'kubo') {
   230→    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   231→    const blob = new Blob([data as any]);
   232→    const formData = new FormData();
   233→    formData.append('file', blob);
   234→    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
   235→    if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Basic ${token}`;
   236→
   237→    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/api/v0/add?pin=true&cid-version=1`, {
   238→      method: 'POST',
   239→      body: formData,
   240→      headers,
   241→      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60_000),
   242→    });
   243→    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Pin to Kubo failed: ${response.status}`);
   244→    const result = (await response.json()) as { Hash: string };
   245→    return result.Hash;
   246→  }
   247→
   248→  // PSA: pin by CID reference (data must already exist on IPFS network)
   249→  const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}/pins`, {
   250→    method: 'POST',
   251→    headers: {
   252→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   253→      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   254→    },
   255→    body: JSON.stringify({
   256→      cid: expectedCid,
   257→      name: `migration-${Date.now()}`,
   258→    }),
   259→    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
   260→  });
   261→  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`PSA pin failed: ${response.status}`);
   262→  const result = (await response.json()) as { pin: { cid: string } };
   263→  return result.pin.cid;
   264→}
   265→
   266→// NOTE: No zeroString function -- JS strings are immutable and cannot be zeroed.
   267→// All credentials are processed as Uint8Array throughout and zeroed with .fill(0)
```

```json
  "content": "/**\n * SSRF Validation Tests\n *\n * Tests for the SSRF protection utilities used by TEE worker routes.\n * Covers private IP ranges, DNS rebinding prevention, redirect blocking,\n * and edge cases from security review REVIEW-2026-03-25-phase-21.\n */\n\nimport { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';\n\n// We need to test the module's exported functions\n// Since isPrivateAddress is not exported, we test it indirectly via validateEndpointUrl and validateResolvedIp\n\ndescribe('ssrf-validation', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.unstubAllEnvs();\n  });\n\n  describe('validateEndpointUrl', () => {\n    let validateEndpointUrl: (endpoint: string) => void;\n\n    beforeEach(async () => {\n      vi.stubEnv('TEE_MODE', 'production');\n      const mod = await import('../services/ssrf-validation.js');\n      validateEndpointUrl = mod.validateEndpointUrl;\n    });\n\n    it('accepts valid HTTPS URLs', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://api.pinata.cloud')).not.toThrow();\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://ipfs.example.com:5001')).not.toThrow();\n    });\n\n    it('rejects HTTP URLs', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('http://api.pinata.cloud')).toThrow('HTTPS');\n    });\n\n    // RFC 1918: 10.0.0.0/8\n    it('rejects 10.x.x.x private range', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://10.0.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://10.255.255.255')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // RFC 1918: 192.168.0.0/16\n    it('rejects 192.168.x.x private range', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://192.168.1.1')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://192.168.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // RFC 1918: 172.16.0.0/12\n    it('rejects 172.16-31.x.x private range', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://172.16.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://172.31.255.255')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    it('accepts 172.15.x.x and 172.32.x.x (outside /12)', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://172.15.0.1')).not.toThrow();\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://172.32.0.1')).not.toThrow();\n    });\n\n    // Loopback\n    it('rejects localhost and 127.x.x.x', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://localhost')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://127.0.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://127.0.0.2')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // Link-local\n    it('rejects 169.254.x.x link-local (AWS metadata)', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://169.254.169.254')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://169.254.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // IPv6\n    it('rejects IPv6 loopback and link-local', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://[::1]')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://[fe80::1]')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // Unique-local IPv6\n    it('rejects fd00::/8 and fc00::/7 unique-local IPv6', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://[fd00::1]')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://[fc00::1]')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // 0.0.0.0\n    it('rejects 0.0.0.0', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://0.0.0.0')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // RFC 6598: CGN range 100.64.0.0/10\n    it('rejects CGN range 100.64-127.x.x', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://100.64.0.1')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://100.127.255.255')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    it('accepts 100.63.x.x and 100.128.x.x (outside CGN)', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://100.63.0.1')).not.toThrow();\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://100.128.0.1')).not.toThrow();\n    });\n\n    // Internal/local suffixes\n    it('rejects .internal and .local suffixes', () => {\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://metadata.internal')).toThrow('private');\n      expect(() => validateEndpointUrl('https://ipfs.local')).toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    // Simulator mode bypasses\n    it('skips validation in simulator mode', async () => {\n      vi.stubEnv('TEE_MODE', 'simulator');\n      const mod = await import('../services/ssrf-validation.js');\n      expect(() => mod.validateEndpointUrl('http://localhost:5001')).not.toThrow();\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('validateResolvedIp', () => {\n    let validateResolvedIp: (hostname: string) => Promise<void>;\n\n    beforeEach(async () => {\n      const mod = await import('../services/ssrf-validation.js');\n      validateResolvedIp = mod.validateResolvedIp;\n    });\n\n    it('rejects hostname resolving to private IP', async () => {\n      // Mock DNS to return private IP\n      const dns = await import('node:dns/promises');\n      vi.spyOn(dns, 'lookup').mockResolvedValue({ address: '169.254.169.254', family: 4 });\n\n      await expect(validateResolvedIp('evil.attacker.com')).rejects.toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    it('rejects IPv4-mapped IPv6 private address', async () => {\n      const dns = await import('node:dns/promises');\n      vi.spyOn(dns, 'lookup').mockResolvedValue({ address: '::ffff:127.0.0.1', family: 6 });\n\n      await expect(validateResolvedIp('evil.attacker.com')).rejects.toThrow('private');\n    });\n\n    it('accepts hostname resolving to public IP', async () => {\n      const dns = await import('node:dns/promises');\n      vi.spyOn(dns, 'lookup').mockResolvedValue({ address: '93.184.216.34', family: 4 });\n\n      await expect(validateResolvedIp('example.com')).resolves.toBeUndefined();\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('ssrfSafeFetch', () => {\n    let ssrfSafeFetch: (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;\n\n    beforeEach(async () => {\n      const mod = await import('../services/ssrf-validation.js');\n      ssrfSafeFetch = mod.ssrfSafeFetch;\n    });\n\n    it('sets redirect to error to prevent redirect-based SSRF', async () => {\n      const mockFetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Response('ok'));\n      vi.stubGlobal('fetch', mockFetch);\n\n      await ssrfSafeFetch('https://example.com/test', { method: 'GET' });\n\n      expect(mockFetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://example.com/test', {\n        method: 'GET',\n        redirect: 'error',\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('preserves other request options', async () => {\n      const mockFetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Response('ok'));\n      vi.stubGlobal('fetch', mockFetch);\n\n      await ssrfSafeFetch('https://example.com', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer token' },\n        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),\n      });\n\n      const callArgs = mockFetch.mock.calls[0][1];\n      expect(callArgs.method).toBe('POST');\n      expect(callArgs.headers).toEqual({ Authorization: 'Bearer token' });\n      expect(callArgs.redirect).toBe('error');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('migrate route batch size limit', () => {\n    // Integration-level test suggestion: verify /migrate rejects > 50 CIDs\n    it.todo('rejects batch sizes exceeding MAX_BATCH_SIZE (50)');\n    it.todo('accepts batch sizes within limit');\n  });\n\n  describe('credential zeroing', () => {\n    it.todo('TEE private key is zeroed after ECIES decryption');\n    it.todo('auth token bytes are zeroed in finally block after migration batch');\n    it.todo('config bytes are zeroed in finally block after connection test');\n  });\n\n  describe('ECIES credential flow', () => {\n    it.todo('connection test rejects malformed hex in encryptedConfig');\n    it.todo('connection test rejects missing epoch parameter');\n    it.todo('migration rejects ECIES decryption with wrong epoch key');\n  });\n});\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-phase-20/tee-worker/src/services/ssrf-validation.ts",
  "old_string": "function isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {\n  // Normalize: strip IPv4-mapped IPv6 prefix (::ffff:127.0.0.1 → 127.0.0.1)\n  const normalized = addr.startsWith('::ffff:') ? addr.slice(7) : addr;",
  "new_string": "function isPrivateAddress(addr: string): boolean {\n  // Strip IPv6 brackets from URL.hostname (e.g. [::1] → ::1)\n  const stripped = addr.startsWith('[') && addr.endsWith(']') ? addr.slice(1, -1) : addr;\n  // Strip IPv4-mapped IPv6 prefix (::ffff:127.0.0.1 → 127.0.0.1)\n  const normalized = stripped.startsWith('::ffff:') ? stripped.slice(7) : stripped;"
}
```

> AGENT

Clean (the vitest types are available at runtime but not in tsc scope — standard for test files in this project).

Here's a summary of everything done:

**HIGH-01 Fixed: SSRF Blocklist Gaps** (`ssrf-validation.ts`)
- Added `0.0.0.0`, `::` (unspecified IPv6)
- Added `fc00::/7` unique-local IPv6 range
- Added `100.64.0.0/10` CGN (RFC 6598) range
- Added IPv4-mapped IPv6 normalization (`::ffff:127.0.0.1` → `127.0.0.1`)
- Added IPv6 bracket stripping for URL.hostname values

**HIGH-02 Fixed: Redirect-based SSRF bypass** (`ssrf-validation.ts`, `connection-test.ts`, `migration-worker.ts`)
- Created `ssrfSafeFetch()` wrapper that sets `redirect: 'error'`
- Replaced all `fetch()` calls in TEE worker with `ssrfSafeFetch()`
- Added `MAX_BATCH_SIZE = 50` validation on `/migrate` route (addresses MEDIUM-03)

**Test file:** `tee-worker/src/__tests__/ssrf-validation.test.ts` — 20 passing tests + 8 todos for integration tests

**Report:** `.planning/security/REVIEW-2026-03-25-phase-21.md`

6px and 2px gaps are pre-existing values outside this contract's scope.\n+\n+**Source:** Existing token scale from `index.css` `:root` block (`--spacing-xs` through `--spacing-xl`).\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Typography\n+\n+| Role            | Size | Weight | Line Height | CSS Token                                  |\n+| --------------- | ---- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------------------ |\n+| Body            | 11px | 400    | 1.5         | `--font-size-sm`, `--font-weight-normal`   |\n+| Status/Label    | 10px | 400    | 1.5         | `--font-size-xs`, `--font-weight-normal`   |\n+| Section Heading | 14px | 600    | 1.5         | `--font-size-md`, `--font-weight-semibold` |\n+| Tab Label       | 10px | 600    | 1.5         | `--font-size-xs`, `--font-weight-semibold` |\n+\n+All text uses `var(--font-family-mono)` (JetBrains Mono). All text is `text-transform: lowercase` or `text-transform: uppercase` per existing patterns -- headings lowercase, tab labels and badges uppercase, body text lowercase.\n+\n+**Source:** Existing > AGENT

Found it. The baseline uses `TIMESTAMP` (without timezone), but the `AddPinMigrations` migration uses `TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE`. TypeORM's `@CreateDateColumn()` maps to `TIMESTAMP` by default. The mismatch causes drift detection.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01GrmdzchhUUxmKgdBusf9ET
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-phase-20/apps/api/src/migrations/1742000000000-AddPinMigrations.ts",
  "old_string": "        created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n        updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n        completed_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE",
  "new_string": "        created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n        updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n        completed_at TIMESTAMP"
}
```

```
    16→import { MigrationStatusDto } from './dto/migration-status.dto';
    17→
    18→@ApiTags('migration')

    21→@Controller('migration')
    22→export class MigrationController {
    23→  constructor(private readonly migrationService: MigrationService) {}
    24→
    25→  @Post('start')
    26→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Start a pin migration between providers' })
    27→  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Migration started' })
    28→  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Active migration already exists' })
    29→  async startMigration(
    30→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
    31→    @Body() dto: StartMigrationDto

    34→    return { migrationId };

    37→  @Get('status')

    39→  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, type: MigrationStatusDto })

    41→    return this.migrationService.getStatus(req.user.id);
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  @Post(':id/pause')

    46→  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Migration paused' })
    47→  async pauseMigration(
    48→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
    49→    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string
    50→  ): Promise<{ message: string }> {
    51→    await this.migrationService.pauseMigration(req.user.id, id);
    52→    return { message: 'Migration paused' };
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  @Post(':id/resume')
    56→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Resume a paused migration' })
    57→  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Migration resumed' })
    58→  async resumeMigration(
    59→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
    60→    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string
    61→  ): Promise<{ message: string }> {
    62→    await this.migrationService.resumeMigration(req.user.id, id);
    63→    return { message: 'Migration resumed' };
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  @Post(':id/cancel')
    67→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Cancel a migration' })

    69→  async cancelMigration(
    70→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,

    73→    await this.migrationService.cancelMigration(req.user.id, id);
    74→    return { message: 'Migration cancelled' };
    75→  }
```

```
     1→import { Processor, WorkerHost } from '@nestjs/bullmq';
     2→import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
     3→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     4→import { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
     5→import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
     6→import { Job } from 'bullmq';
     7→import { PinMigration } from './migration.entity';
     8→import { PinnedCid } from '../vault/entities/pinned-cid.entity';
     9→import { MigrationService } from './migration.service';
    10→
    11→const BATCH_SIZE = 10;
    12→
    13→@Processor('pin-migration')
    14→export class MigrationProcessor extends WorkerHost {
    15→  private readonly logger = new Logger(MigrationProcessor.name);
    16→
    17→  constructor(
    18→    @InjectRepository(PinMigration)
    19→    private readonly migrationRepo: Repository<PinMigration>,
    20→    @InjectRepository(PinnedCid)
    21→    private readonly pinnedCidRepo: Repository<PinnedCid>,
    22→    private readonly migrationService: MigrationService,
    23→    private readonly configService: ConfigService
    24→  ) {
    25→    super();
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  async process(job: Job<{ migrationId: string }>): Promise<void> {
    29→    const { migrationId } = job.data;
    30→
    31→    const migration = await this.migrationRepo.findOneOrFail({
    32→      where: { id: migrationId },
    33→    });
    34→
    35→    // Mark as running
    36→    if (migration.status === 'pending') {
    37→      await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, { status: 'running' });
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    // Get all CIDs for user
    41→    const pinnedCids = await this.pinnedCidRepo.find({
    42→      where: { userId: migration.userId },
    43→    });
    44→
    45→    const TEE_URL = this.configService.get<string>('TEE_WORKER_URL');
    46→    const TEE_SECRET = this.configService.get<string>('TEE_WORKER_SECRET');
    47→
    48→    if (!TEE_URL || !TEE_SECRET) {
    49→      this.logger.error('TEE_WORKER_URL or TEE_WORKER_SECRET not configured');
    50→      await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, { status: 'failed' });
    51→      return;
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    this.logger.log(
    55→      `Processing migration ${migrationId}: ${pinnedCids.length} CIDs in batches of ${BATCH_SIZE}`
    56→    );
    57→
    58→    for (let i = 0; i < pinnedCids.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
    59→      // Check if paused or cancelled before processing each batch
    60→      const current = await this.migrationRepo.findOneOrFail({
    61→        where: { id: migrationId },
    62→      });
    63→      if (current.status === 'paused' || current.status === 'cancelled') {
    64→        this.logger.log(`Migration ${migrationId} ${current.status} -- stopping processing`);
    65→        break;
    66→      }
    67→
    68→      const batch = pinnedCids.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
    69→      const cids = batch.map((p) => p.cid);
    70→
    71→      try {
    72→        const response = await fetch(`${TEE_URL}/migrate`, {
    73→          method: 'POST',
    74→          headers: {
    75→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    76→            Authorization: `Bearer ${TEE_SECRET}`,
    77→          },
    78→          body: JSON.stringify({
    79→            cids,
    80→            sourceConfigEncrypted: migration.sourceConfigEncrypted,
    81→            destConfigEncrypted: migration.destConfigEncrypted,
    82→          }),
    83→        });
    84→
    85→        if (!response.ok) {
    86→          const text = await response.text();
    87→
    88→          // Handle 401 specifically (token expired or invalid TEE auth)
    89→          if (response.status === 401) {
    90→            await this.migrationService.updateProgress(migrationId, 0, cids.length, cids);
    91→            await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, { status: 'paused' });
    92→            this.logger.warn(`Migration ${migrationId} paused due to TEE auth failure (401)`);
    93→            return;
    94→          }
    95→
    96→          throw new Error(`TEE migration failed: ${response.status} ${text}`);
    97→        }
    98→
    99→        const result = (await response.json()) as {
   100→          succeeded?: string[];
   101→          failed?: string[];
   102→        };
   103→
   104→        await this.migrationService.updateProgress(
   105→          migrationId,
   106→          result.succeeded?.length ?? 0,
   107→          result.failed?.length ?? 0,
   108→          result.failed
   109→        );
   110→      } catch (err) {
   111→        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   112→        this.logger.error(`Migration ${migrationId} batch error at offset ${i}: ${message}`);
   113→        await this.migrationService.updateProgress(migrationId, 0, cids.length, cids);
   114→      }
   115→    }
   116→
   117→    // Mark complete if still running
   118→    const final = await this.migrationRepo.findOneOrFail({
   119→      where: { id: migrationId },
   120→    });
   121→    if (final.status === 'running') {
   122→      await this.migrationRepo.update(migrationId, {
   123→        status: 'completed',
   124→        completedAt: new Date(),
   125→      });
   126→      this.logger.log(`Migration ${migrationId} completed`);
   127→    }
   128→  }
   129→}
   130→
```

Body: Cleanup (unpin) won’t run if any step after pinning throws (e.g., ipns-publish failure), which can leave orphaned pins on external providers and skew load test results/costs. Consider moving cleanup into a finally block that runs whenever a CID was successfully obtained, and in PSA mode also ensure PSA cleanup is attempted even if relay unpin fails.

```suggestion
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(
        `[Client ${pc.id}] BYO file ${i} failed: ${(err as Error).message?.slice(0, 150)}`
      );

      // Cleanup: unpin from external provider / PSA whenever we have a CID
```

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM52mHGH ===
File: packages/sdk/src/types.ts:21
Comment ID: 2985445796
Body: As typed, PinningConfig allows mode='external'|'dual' without externalProvider, but the runtime logic silently falls back to cipherbox behavior when externalProvider is missing. Consider making PinningConfig a discriminated union that requires externalProvider when mode !== 'cipherbox' so misconfiguration is caught at compile time (and potentially validated at runtime for SDK consumers coming from JSON).
```suggestion
type NonCipherboxPinningMode = Exclude<PinningMode, 'cipherbox'>;

export type PinningConfig =
  | {
      /** All pins go through CipherBox infrastructure only. */
      mode: 'cipherbox';
      /** No external provider is used in cipherbox-only mode. */
      externalProvider?: undefined;
    }
  | {
      /** Pins are sent to an external provider (or both CipherBox + external). */
      mode: NonCipherboxPinningMode;
      /** Configuration for the external pinning provider (required when mode !== 'cipherbox'). */
      externalProvider: ExternalProviderConfig;
    };
```

```
=== COMMENT 2985479498 ===
File: packages/sdk/src/client.ts:1079
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_

[analysis details omitted]

**Don't drop the relay copy before the PSA pin is confirmed durable.**

The code treats `pinByCid()` resolution as success and immediately unpins the relay copy, but PSA returns 'queued' status—meaning the service has only *accepted* the request, not confirmed it has fetched and pinned the content. Unpinning before PSA completes its async fetch can cause data loss. Additionally, unpinFromIpfs failures are silently swallowed, maskin

=== COMMENT 2985479508 ===
File: tee-worker/src/services/migration-worker.ts:99
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_

[analysis details omitted]

**Fix DNS rebinding TOCTOU gap: pin the validated IP for actual fetch.**

The endpoint validation at lines 88-99 resolves hostnames separately from the actual fetch operations. This creates a TOCTOU vulnerability: between `validateResolvedIp()` (which resolves DNS once) and `ssrfSafeFetch()` at fetch time (which performs a fresh DNS resolution), an attacker can rebind the domain to a different IP.

The `redirect: 'error'` blocking 

=== COMMENT 2985479486 ===
File: apps/web/src/components/settings/MigrationProgress.tsx:40
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Don't tear down polling when the status endpoint returns `null`.**

Line 35 clears the interval for the no-migration case, but nothing in this component starts it again after mount. If the user lands on STORAGE with no active job and then starts a migration from the same mounted page, this component will stay `null` until it remounts.

[analysis details omitted]

=== COMMENT 2985479487 ===
File: apps/web/src/components/settings/MigrationProgress.tsx:45
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Avoid `setInterval` with an async poller.**

Line 45 schedules a new poll every 5 seconds even if the previous `fetchStatus()` call is still in flight. Slow responses can overlap and apply out-of-order state updates around pause/resume/cancel transitions. A single-flight recursive `setTimeout` is safer here.

[analysis details omitted]

=== COMMENT 2985479489 ===
File: apps/web/src/hooks/useAuth.ts:250
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Remove or namespace the BYO IPNS cache.**

`cipherbox-byo-ipns-name` is global to the browser. Once one account writes it, a different account on the same device will reuse the stale IPNS name, fail decryption, hit the catch path, and never overwrite the cached value because `storedIpnsName` is already set.

=== COMMENT 2985479481 ===
File: apps/api/src/migration/migration.processor.ts:83
Body: _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Bound the TEE worker call with a timeout.**

This does a raw `fetch()` to `${TEE_URL}/migrate` with no abort/timeout. If the TEE worker hangs or the network stalls, the job can sit in `running` forever and tie up a BullMQ worker slot. Please add a timeout and convert that path into an explicit pause/failure transition.
```

```json
  "new_string": "  const TERMINAL_STATUSES = ['completed', 'failed', 'cancelled'];\n\n  const fetchStatus = useCallback(async () => {\n    const status = await migrationApi.getStatus();\n    // Only update state if values actually changed to avoid no-op re-renders\n    setMigration((prev) => {\n      if (!status && !prev) return prev;\n      if (\n        prev &&\n        status &&\n        prev.status === status.status &&\n        prev.migratedCids === status.migratedCids &&\n        prev.failedCids === status.failedCids\n      ) {\n        return prev;\n      }\n      return status;\n    });\n    return status;\n  }, []);\n\n  // Single-flight recursive setTimeout avoids overlapping polls\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    async function poll() {\n      const status = await fetchStatus();\n      if (cancelled) return;\n      // Stop polling on terminal state; keep polling on null (migration may start)\n      if (status && TERMINAL_STATUSES.includes(status.status)) return;\n      pollRef.current = setTimeout(poll, 5000);\n    }\n    poll();\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n      if (pollRef.current) clearTimeout(pollRef.current);\n    };\n  }, [fetchStatus]);"
}
```

> AGENT

The IPNS name itself is already unique per user (derived from their private key via HKDF). The localStorage key is just a cache to avoid re-derivation. But the concern is valid — if two accounts share a browser, the cached name could point to the wrong account's IPNS record. The simplest fix: use the IPNS name derived from the keypair as the source of truth (it's already there), and only use localStorage as a fallback hint. Actually, the current code already does this — it uses `byoKeypair.ipnsName` as the fallback when localStorage is empty. The localStorage is just a speed optimization to avoid IPNS resolution on a name that might differ.

The real fix is to just always use `byoKeypair.ipnsName` (which is per-user deterministic) and skip localStorage caching entirely. It's a micro-optimization that causes a real multi-account bug.

> TOOL

```
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Use a stable CID snapshot instead of the live pin set.**

Line 41 reloads the current `PinnedCid` rows, and Line 58 always restarts batching from index `0`. `MigrationService.updateProgress()` only persists aggregate counters, so a paused/resumed or delayed job will re-send already processed CIDs, double-count progress, and can turn already-migrated CIDs into false failures after the source pin has been removed. Batch against a migration-owned snapshot/cursor 

=== COMMENT 2988942661 ===
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_



**Add a time bound to the BYO config lookup to prevent indefinite login blocking.**

The BYO lookup at lines 234–238 and its await at line 267 currently have no explicit timeout. If `resolveIpnsRecord()` or `fetchFromIpfs()` hit a slow or hung IPFS peer, the login spinner can wait indefinitely instead of promptly falling back to cipherbox-only mode (which is the intended graceful degradation).

Wrap `loadByoConfig()` with an explicit timeout (e.g., 10–15s usin

=== COMMENT 2988942670 ===
_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Validate the decrypted BYO blob before constructing `PinningConfig`.**

`JSON.parse(json) as ByoIpfsConfig` is compile-time only. A stale/corrupt blob that still parses as JSON can reach this return path with an invalid shape (`pinningMode` missing, or an external mode without a provider) and initialize the SDK with an impossible pinning state. Add a runtime guard/schema check here and fall back to `undefined` when the payload shape is invalid.
```

```json
  "old_string": "      const loadByoConfig = async (\n        userPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n      ): Promise<PinningConfig | undefined> => {\n        try {\n          const byoKeypair = await deriveByoConfigIpnsKeypair(userPrivateKey);\n          // Always use the derived IPNS name (per-user deterministic) — no localStorage\n          // caching to avoid stale cross-account leakage on shared browsers\n          const ipnsName = byoKeypair.ipnsName;\n\n          const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName);\n          if (!resolved?.cid) return undefined;\n\n          const encrypted = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);\n          const plaintext = await unwrapKey(encrypted, userPrivateKey);\n          let config: ByoIpfsConfig;\n          try {\n            const json = new TextDecoder().decode(plaintext);\n            config = JSON.parse(json) as ByoIpfsConfig;\n          } finally {\n            clearBytes(plaintext);\n          }\n\n          return {\n            mode: config.pinningMode,\n            externalProvider: config.externalProvider ?? undefined,\n          };\n        } catch {\n          // No BYO config or decryption failed -- default to cipherbox-only\n          return undefined;\n        }\n      };",
  "new_string": "      const loadByoConfig = async (\n        userPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n      ): Promise<PinningConfig | undefined> => {\n        // Time-bound the entire lookup so a hung IPFS peer can't block login\n        const BYO_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n        const inner = async (): Promise<PinningConfig | undefined> => {\n          const byoKeypair = await deriveByoConfigIpnsKeypair(userPrivateKey);\n          const ipnsName = byoKeypair.ipnsName;\n\n          const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName);\n          if (!resolved?.cid) return undefined;\n\n          const encrypted = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);\n          const plaintext = await unwrapKey(encrypted, userPrivateKey);\n          let config: ByoIpfsConfig;\n          try {\n            const json = new TextDecoder().decode(plaintext);\n            config = JSON.parse(json) as ByoIpfsConfig;\n          } finally {\n            clearBytes(plaintext);\n          }\n\n          // Runtime validation: ensure the blob has a valid shape\n          const validModes = ['cipherbox', 'external', 'dual'];\n          if (!config.pinningMode || !validModes.includes(config.pinningMode)) {\n            return undefined;\n          }\n          if (config.pinningMode !== 'cipherbox' && !config.externalProvider?.endpoint) {\n            return undefined;\n          }\n\n          return {\n            mode: config.pinningMode,\n            externalProvider: config.externalProvider ?? undefined,\n          };\n        };\n\n        try {\n          const result = await Promise.race([\n            inner(),\n            new Promise<undefined>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(undefined), BYO_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS)),\n          ]);\n          return result;\n        } catch {\n          // No BYO config or decryption failed -- default to cipherbox-only\n          return undefined;\n        }\n      };"
}
```
